Result: 随着磁耦合增强，部分初值下轨道由规则转为混沌（表现为散乱Poincaré截面与持续正的FTLE）；高密度暗物质晕使FTLE增大，弱晕修正在数值上不可靠；另有一组初值仍呈现闭合Poincaré曲线与衰减的FTLE，表明规则运动可在非零磁耦合下持续存在。

Conclusion: King型暗物质晕以初始条件依赖的方式调制磁场诱导的轨道不稳定性，但规则运动在磁化系统中依然存在，说明该系统兼具混沌与规则共存的特性。

Abstract: We investigate the dynamics of charged test particles around a rotating black hole embedded in a King-type dark matter halo and subjected to a prescribed, asymptotically uniform magnetic field. We adopt an effective rotating geometry constructed from a static halo seed through a modified Newman--Janis prescription and a test electromagnetic field that generally requires supporting currents in the nonvacuum background. In the absence of magnetic coupling, the timelike Hamilton--Jacobi equation separates and admits a Carter-type constant, providing an integrable reference system. We characterize the orbital dynamics using Poincaré sections and tangent-vector growth diagnostics, including fast Lyapunov indicators and finite-time Lyapunov exponents, with mass-shell monitoring and numerical refinement checks. For the sampled initial conditions, increasing the magnetic coupling can produce a transition from regular motion to scattered Poincaré sections with sustained positive finite-time growth rates. At fixed magnetic coupling, the two higher-density halo cases yield larger finite-time exponents for a representative chaotic orbit family, whereas the weak-halo correction is not resolved consistently under numerical refinement. A different initial condition yields a closed Poincaré curve and a decreasing finite-time exponent over an extended integration, supporting regular motion despite the nonzero magnetic coupling. These results show that the King-type halo modulates magnetic-field-induced orbital instability in an initial-condition-dependent manner, while regular motion persists within the magnetized system.

</details>


### [99] [Exact solutions of Maxwell vacuum equations in null homogeneous Petrov spaces with solvable motions groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04522)
*V. V. Obukhov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了零齐次Petrov空间 $V^*_4(N)$ 的几何与电磁场结构，通过其对应的三维齐次Riemann空间 $V_3(N)$ 及其Bianchi分类下的三参数运动群 $G_3(N)$ 建立规范标架，导出度规与不变电磁势，并求解真空Maxwell方程；对可解群 $G_3(I)$–$G_3(VII)$ 给出全部非等价显式解，为后续求解Einstein-Maxwell方程大幅简化为常微分方程组。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立零齐次Petrov空间 $V^*_4(N)$ 中不变电磁场的系统构造方法，并求解其真空Maxwell方程，为耦合引力-电磁场（Einstein-Maxwell）系统的求解提供简化路径。

Method: 利用Bianchi分类中三参数运动群 $G_3(N)$ 作用下的三维齐次Riemann空间 $V_3(N)$ 的规范标架，构造四维空间 $V^*_4(N)$ 的度规张量和电磁矢势分量；将真空Maxwell方程表示为仅含群结构常数、非完整度规分量、矢势及其关于波变量导数的形式；对可解群 $G_3(I)$ 至 $G_3(VII)$ 分类求解。

Result: 得到了所有非等价的真空Maxwell方程精确解，且所有解均以波变量的任意独立函数显式表达；由此，Einstein-Maxwell方程组的求解被约化为仅含这些函数的常微分方程组。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了基于Bianchi分类的齐次背景上不变电磁场的完整解析框架，显著简化了Einstein-Maxwell系统的求解难度，为寻找新的精确引力-电磁解提供了系统工具。

Abstract: Null homogeneous Petrov spaces $V^*_4(N)$ are considered. The geometry of $V^*_4(N)$ is determined by the geometry of the three-dimensional homogeneous Riemannian space $V_3(N)$, which is invariant under the action of the three-parameter motions group $G_3(N)$ ($N$ corresponds to the group number in the Bianchi classification). The canonical frame of the space $V_3(N)$ is used to construct the metric tensor of the space $V^*_4(N)$ and the components of the vector potential of the invariant electromagnetic field. Using this reper, we obtain Maxwell vacuum equations containing only the structure constants of the groups, the non-holonomic components of the contravariant metric tensor and vector potential, and their derivatives with respect to the wave variable. All non-equivalent exact solutions of Maxwell vacuum equations are found for the case of solvable motions groups $G_3(I)-G_3(VII)$.
  All solutions of Maxwell vacuum equations obtained in this article are written explicitly in terms of arbitrary independent functions of wave variable. Therefore, when integrating the Einstein-Maxwell equations, it has to consider only the remaining systems of Einstein equations which reduce to systems of ordinary differential equations that include only these independent functions.

</details>


### [100] [Dynamic Quantum Wormholes: Path-Integral State Selection and the Quantum Throat Trajectory in an Exactly Solvable Thin-Shell Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04577)
*Rikpratik Sengupta,Paulo Vargas Moniz*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个球对称薄壳虫洞的minisuperspace量子化模型，基于导波量子宇宙学方法，将喉部半径作为唯一自由度；发现对于无压壳层，Wheeler-DeWitt方程精确化为Whittaker方程，并在质量趋于零时退化为修正Bessel方程；在渐近平直外部时空下，波函数在喉部半径趋于无穷时的有界性选择出唯一实态，导致Bohmian流为零、喉部冻结；该态是正则化Lorentz路径积分在ε→0⁺极限下的精确解；通过Whittaker连接公式解决了近喉部自伴性歧义；当外部时空替换为Schwarzschild-de Sitter时，势能在大喉部半径处趋于负无穷，从而允许存在非零Bohmian流的动态纯出射态，描述一个可通过隧穿进入无界膨胀的长寿命亚稳态虫洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解动态球对称薄壳虫洞的量子行为，特别是其是否可能具有非冻结、动态演化的量子态，以及如何在量子层面处理边界条件、自伴性歧义和路径积分正则化等问题。

Method: 采用minisuperspace量子化与导波（Bohmian）诠释相结合的方法，以喉部半径a(τ)为唯一自由度，求解Israel拼接条件导出的Wheeler-DeWitt方程；利用Whittaker方程与修正Bessel方程的解析性质；结合Resolvent解析性与Picard–Lefschetz柄形分析验证路径积分极限；使用Whittaker及Airy连接公式处理自伴延拓与渐近匹配。

Result: 在渐近平直外部下，唯一物理可接受态为实态，Bohmian流恒为零，喉部完全冻结；该态精确对应ε→0⁺极限下的正则化Lorentz路径积分；在Schwarzschild–de Sitter外部下，存在非零Bohmian流的纯出射动态态，支持长寿命亚稳态虫洞及其隧穿至无界膨胀，隧穿率与WKB估计一致；Airy连接给出残余常数κ_∞≈0.814。

Conclusion: 虫洞的量子动力学强烈依赖于外部时空几何：渐近平直性导致量子冻结，而正宇宙常数（dS）则解除该限制，使动态隧穿演化成为可能；导波诠释能清晰揭示经典禁止区内的量子流行为；解析方法（Whittaker/Airy连接、柄形分析）可高精度（48位）验证量子宇宙学构造的自洽性。

Abstract: We construct a minisuperspace quantization of a dynamic, spherically symmetric thin-shell wormhole in the spirit of pilot-wave quantum cosmology, treating the throat radius $a(τ)$ as the single degree of freedom of the Israel junction-condition constraint. For a pressureless shell of mass $M$ the Wheeler--DeWitt equation reduces exactly to Whittaker's equation, and to the modified Bessel equation as $M\to0$. When the exterior is asymptotically flat, boundedness of the wave function as $a\to\infty$ selects a unique state that can always be chosen real. Since any real solution has a vanishing Bohmian current, the throat is frozen for any linear equation of state and any operator ordering, independently of the choice between Euclidean and Lorentzian boundary conditions. This state is the exact $ε\to0^+$ limit of the regularized Lorentzian path integral, as shown by resolvent analyticity and by an exact Picard--Lefschetz thimble calculation that agrees with the closed-form solution to $48$ digits. The near-throat self-adjointness ambiguity is resolved in closed form through the Whittaker connection formula. Replacing the exterior by Schwarzschild--de~Sitter removes the obstruction: the potential tends to $-\infty$ at large $a$, and the equation admits a genuinely dynamic, purely outgoing state. Propagated exactly through the classically forbidden barrier, it carries a conserved, nonzero Bohmian current everywhere, including the inner pocket. It describes a long-lived metastable wormhole that tunnels into unbounded expansion at a rate consistent with the WKB estimate. Airy connection formulas leave a residual prefactor constant $κ_\infty=0.814\pm0.004$ for sub-critical coupling, which we do not trace to its analytic origin, and the physical results do not depend on it.

</details>


### [101] [Quasinormal-Mode Collider Spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04595)
*Mian Zhu,Jun Tao,Peng Wang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为‘准正规模碰撞谱学’（QCS）的新框架，用于研究黑洞非线性衰减过程中不同准正规模之间的相互作用，并建立了关于三次应变残差因子化与中间极点重构的两个结构性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统黑洞谱学将准正规模视为独立线性振荡，忽略了非线性环降中模式间相互作用的信息；本文旨在通过类比宇宙学对撞机物理，构建能揭示这些非线性相互作用及中介态的新理论框架。

Method: 基于广义相对论中的黑洞微扰理论，引入准正规模对撞谱学（QCS）框架；推导并证明了在孤立、非简并中间极点处三次应变残差的因子化性质，以及中间极点残差可由视界与无穷远辐射联合重构的结论。

Result: 1）三次应变残差在中间极点处可分解为激发振幅与二次转换系数的乘积，从而对输出通道和二阶初值选择施加秩约束；2）中间极点残差可由视界与无穷远辐射唯一确定，并可外推至全三次波形差。

Conclusion: QCS为探测非线性环降的相互作用结构提供了新范式，实现了低阶辐射与高阶可观测量之间的系统性关联，拓展了强场引力检验的能力。

Abstract: Black-hole (BH) quasinormal modes encode the properties of merger remnants and provide powerful tests of strong-field gravity. The BH spectroscopy has predominantly treated them as independent linear oscillations. Nonlinear ringdown can additionally reveal how these modes interact and which intermediate states mediate their interactions. Inspired by cosmological collider physics, we introduce quasinormal-mode collider spectroscopy (QCS), a framework for organizing and predicting nonlinear BH responses. We establish two structural results. First, at an isolated, nondegenerate intermediate pole, a cubic strain residue factorizes into an excitation amplitude and a quadratic conversion coefficient. This factorization imposes rank constraints across output channels and choices of second-order initial data, providing consistency tests of the intermediate-mode description. Second, the intermediate pole residue can be reconstructed from its horizon and infinity radiation. With the first-order field and third-order data held fixed, this reconstruction extends to the full cubic waveform difference generated by changing the second-order initial data. QCS thus connects lower-order radiation to higher-order observables and provides a framework for probing the interaction structure of nonlinear ringdown.

</details>


### [102] [Covariance scaling and a dual-mode torque diagnostic in measurements of the gravitational constant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04707)
*Vineet Kumar*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文对CODATA-2018中16个G值测量结果进行协方差尺度建模分析，发现历史数据存在显著超出报告不确定度的离散性；通过排除具有装置谱系关联的BIPM-14结果，得到缩放因子λ̂=2.967；NIST新结果在引入历史推断的额外离散性后变得统计上合理；同时，NIST四组力矩差数据支持统一绝对偏移模型而非比例缩放模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决不同实验室测得的万有引力常数G值之间互不一致、且差异超出其宣称不确定度的问题，检验历史数据集内部一致性及新测量结果的可靠性。

Method: 采用比例协方差尺度模型，保留全部报告协方差矩阵；基于装置谱系排除BIPM-14数据以构建主历史拟合；对NIST新结果进行缩放敏感性检验；对比分析NIST四组自由-伺服力矩差，分别拟合绝对偏移与比例缩放两类扭矩模型。

Result: 历史十五个测量的协方差缩放因子λ̂=2.967；NIST新结果在应用该缩放后统计显著性改善（p=0.382）；四组力矩差更支持统一绝对扭矩偏移ΔN̂=1.918±0.215 pN·m，而非比例模型（Δχ²=11.041）。

Conclusion: CODATA-2018中G值测量存在未被报告的系统性离散；NIST新结果与历史数据集在考虑额外分散后一致；提出可用于双模扭秤实验验证的扭矩诊断方法；未建议更新G的推荐值。

Abstract: Laboratory determinations of the Newtonian gravitational constant $G$ disagree by more than their quoted uncertainties allow. We examine the sixteen determinations comprising the CODATA-2018 archive using a proportional covariance-scale model that retains the full reported covariance matrix. Because the 2026 NIST torsion-balance redetermination shares apparatus lineage with the BIPM Mark II balance, our primary historical fit excludes the BIPM-14 determination, giving $\hatλ = 2.967$ for the remaining fifteen measurements. Evaluated against this historical model under the declared uniform covariance-scaling convention, the correlated four-configuration NIST result is highly unlikely under the archive's reported, unscaled covariance but is statistically unremarkable once the historically inferred extra dispersion is applied ($Q_λ= 4.840$; finite-sample $F$ tail $p = 0.382$); a mean-only scaling sensitivity leaves the vector discrepant. Retaining the complete archive also gives a comparable result. We further examine four matched free-minus-servo torque differences reported by NIST. They are consistent with a single common absolute torque offset across the four tested configurations, $\widehat{ΔN} = 1.918 \pm 0.215$ pN m, and poorly described by an equally simple fractional-scale alternative under the stated independent-Type-A treatment, with a chi-square contrast of $Δχ^{2} = 11.041$ between the two torque models. The paper provides a covariance audit of the historical archive, a lineage-qualified consistency check, and an experimentally testable diagnostic for dual-mode torsion balances, without proposing a revised value of $G$.

</details>


### [103] [Polyakov backreaction in large D discretely self-similar collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04960)
*Sheng-Hong Lai,Wen-Yu Wen*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了高维爱因斯坦-克莱因-戈登理论中离散自相似解的反常诱导半经典反作用，发现最小耦合Polyakov模型无法给出经典中心的一般光滑、精确自相似延拓。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解高维引力理论中量子反常对自相似时空结构的影响，特别是其对中心奇点和视界正则性的修正作用。

Method: 基于大维度展开，结合Floquet理论、球对称约化、Polyakov辅助场方法及非线性内区问题构造，分析反常导致的能动量边界分离与临界标量场曲面。

Result: 发现Polyakov辅助场具有仿周期性而非周期性；识别出一个临界膨胀子曲面，其一般穿越导致奇点，仅特定高曲率喉区可匹配到正则零质量外解；反常使有效类光能量边界偏离经典物质流与零曲率曲线。

Conclusion: 最小耦合Polyakov模型不能提供经典中心的一般光滑、精确自相似完成，暗示需更复杂的量子引力效应或耦合机制来实现正则性。

Abstract: We study anomaly-induced semiclassical backreaction on an analytic family of discretely self-similar solutions of Einstein--Klein--Gordon theory at large spacetime dimension. Horizon regularity fixes a null-coordinate Floquet exponent and selects a unique regular quantum state. The Polyakov auxiliary field is affine-periodic rather than periodic, with a monodromy that is determined independently by a global zero-mode condition and by the conformal-coordinate scaling. Spherical reduction introduces a quantum coupling that grows toward the center, so the periodic first-order expansion is nonuniform. We construct the resulting nonlinear inner problem and find a critical dilaton surface. Generic crossings of this surface are singular, whereas matching to the regular zero-mass exterior selects a narrow, high-curvature throat. The anomaly also separates the effective null-energy boundaries from the classical matter flux and zero-curvature curves. These results show that the minimally coupled Polyakov model does not yield a generic smooth, exactly self-similar completion of the classical center.

</details>


### [104] [Thermodynamic Volumes of Extended Black Hole Thermodynamics with Auxiliary Gauge Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05022)
*Zili Mou,Yu Tian,Hongbao Zhang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过Iyer-Wald形式主义，将引力系统中原本为常数的耦合参数（如宇宙学常数）提升为动力学标量场，并引入相应辅助规范场，推导出扩展黑洞热力学第一定律；所得热力学体积作为耦合参数的共轭变量，与辅助规范场无关，且与先前‘暴力变分’方法结果一致；同时证明该体积亦可用辅助规范场在黑洞视界上表达并计算，验证了先前文献中的相关提议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为统一处理引力理论中耦合常数（如宇宙学常数）作为热力学变量的问题，避免传统‘暴力变分’方法的不严格性，需建立更基础、协变的热力学第一定律推导框架。

Method: 采用Iyer-Wald形式主义，将原拉格朗日量中的耦合常数动态化为标量场，并引入辅助规范场构造修正拉格朗日量，在此框架下推导黑洞热力学第一定律及共轭热力学体积。

Result: 得到与辅助规范场无关的热力学体积，其值与此前暴力变分法结果完全一致；进一步证明该体积可等价表示为辅助规范场在黑洞视界上的特定组合并可显式计算。

Conclusion: Iyer-Wald形式主义能自洽地导出扩展黑洞热力学第一定律，确认热力学体积的几何本质与规范无关性，并从原理上支持将耦合常数视为热力学变量的物理合理性。

Abstract: By applying the Iyer-Wald formalism to the modified Lagrangian form for the gravitational system, where the coupling constants appearing in the original Lagrangian form, such as the cosmological constant, are promoted to the dynamical scalar fields by introducing the corresponding auxiliary gauge fields, we derive the first law of the extended black hole thermodynamics, where the resulting thermodynamic volumes as the conjugate thermodynamic variables to the coupling constants are nevertheless independent of the auxiliary gauge fields and exactly the same as those obtained before via varying the coupling constants by brute force. In addition, it is further shown that such volumes can also be expressed in terms of the auxiliary gauge fields and evaluated on the black hole horizon, validating the proposal made in the previous literature.

</details>


### [105] [Thermodynamic Stability of a Viscous Variable Generalised Chaplygin Gas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05361)
*D. Panigrahi,K. Kanjilal,Saibal Ray*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了粘性可变广义查普利金气体（VVGCG）模型在平坦FLRW宇宙中的宇宙学动力学与热力学性质，发现其能一致描述从早期减速膨胀到晚期幻影加速的宇宙演化，并在整个演化过程中保持热力学稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为构建一个既能解释宇宙早期减速膨胀、又能描述晚期加速膨胀，同时满足热力学基本定律和稳定性条件的统一暗能量-暗物质模型。

Method: 基于状态方程 $P=-B/ρ^α$（其中 $B=B_0V^{n/3}$）和粘性压强 $Π=-3ξH$（$ξ=ξ_0ρ^{1/2}$），在平坦FLRW度规下分析宇宙动力学演化；结合热力学第一、第二及第三定律，系统推导并检验熵、热容、热/量热态方程、等温与绝热稳定性等热力学量。

Result: 确定物理可行分支为 $ξ_0<0$，存在由减速转加速的体积阈值 $V_f$；大体积下状态方程参量趋近幻影区（$n<0$）；热力学分析表明 $n<0$ 且 $ξ_0<0$ 时稳定性与正定热容 $c_V>0$ 同时成立；熵在 $T→0$ 时趋于零，符合第三定律；模型存在有限最小体积与最大温度；Hubble-57 数据约束得 $α=0.0024$, $n=-0.0182$, $ξ_0=-0.0015$，落在热力学稳定区。

Conclusion: VVGCG模型不仅统一描述了宇宙从减速到幻影加速的完整演化历史，而且在全演化过程中严格满足热力学一致性与稳定性，是具有坚实物理基础的暗能量候选模型。

Abstract: We investigate the cosmological dynamics and thermodynamic properties of a viscous variable generalized Chaplygin gas (VVGCG) model in a spatially flat Friedmann--Lema^ıtre--Robertson--Walker universe, described by $P=-B/ρ^α$, where $B=B_0V^{n/3}$. The model includes a bulk-viscous pressure $Π=-3ξH$, with $ξ=ξ_0ρ^{1/2}$. We find that the physically viable branch corresponds to $ξ_0<0$, for which the positive viscous pressure dominates at early times, producing decelerated expansion. As the volume increases, the effective pressure changes sign at a finite volume $V_c$, while the transition to acceleration occurs at a larger volume $V_f$. At large volume, the equation-of-state parameter approaches a phantom-like regime for $n<0$. A detailed thermodynamic analysis of the thermal and caloric equations of state, entropy, heat capacity, and isothermal and adiabatic stability shows that the stability conditions and $c_V>0$ are simultaneously satisfied for $n<0$ and $ξ_0<0$. The entropy vanishes as $T\rightarrow0$, consistent with the third law, while the model admits a finite minimum volume and corresponding maximum temperature. Thermodynamic consistency is further verified through the fundamental thermodynamic identity. The framework also contains several existing Chaplygin-gas models as special cases. Constraints from the Hubble-57 data yield $α=0.0024$, $n=-0.0182$, and $ξ_0=-0.0015$, consistent with the thermodynamically stable parameter region. Thus, the VVGCG model provides a consistent description of cosmic evolution from an early decelerating phase to late-time phantom-like acceleration while remaining thermodynamically stable throughout the evolution.

</details>


### [106] [Ellis-Bronnikov wormholes with NUT charge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05656)
*Masato Nozawa,Tomohiro Harada,Keisuke Nakashi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构建了爱因斯坦-幻影标量场系统中带NUT荷的Ellis-Bronnikov虫洞的精确解，该解是定态、局部渐近平坦（或AdS）且无曲率奇点的；它继承了Taub-NUT几何的若干特征（如Dirac-Misner弦和闭合类时曲线），但因无Killing视界而保证测地完备性；作者还通过引入具多重AdS真空的标量势，得到连接不同真空的渐近AdS虫洞，并用协变相空间方法证明其哈密顿荷有限，最后指出其欧氏化后度规共形类具有ambi-Kähler结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构造具有NUT荷、无奇点、测地完备且兼具物理合理性的虫洞时空解，并拓展至渐近AdS情形以探索全息对偶等应用可能。

Method: 基于爱因斯坦-幻影标量场系统求解精确引力场方程；采用协变相空间形式主义计算哈密顿荷；通过双Wick转动进行欧氏化分析，并研究其共形几何结构（ambi-Kähler）。

Result: 获得两个新精确解：(1) 定态、渐近局部平坦、无奇点、测地完备的NUT型虫洞；(2) 渐近局部AdS、连接不同AdS真空的NUT虫洞；证明其哈密顿荷在适当标量边界条件下有限；欧氏化后度规共形类具有ambi-Kähler结构。

Conclusion: NUT荷可与虫洞结构相容，且不破坏测地完备性；引入合适标量势可实现AdS虫洞并连接不同真空；该类解在经典引力与几何结构层面具有丰富性质，为量子引力与全息研究提供新模型。

Abstract: We construct an exact NUT-charged generalization of the Ellis-Bronnikov wormhole in the Einstein-phantom-scalar system. The solution is stationary, asymptotically locally flat at both ends and free of curvature singularities. It inherits characteristic features of the Taub-NUT geometry, including the Dirac-Misner string and closed timelike curves, while the absence of Killing horizons eliminates the horizon-induced infinite winding of geodesics and renders the spacetime geodesically complete. By allowing the phantom field to have a nontrivial scalar potential with an infinite sequence of AdS vacua, we also obtain an exact asymptotically locally AdS wormhole with a NUT charge, which interpolates between different vacua at its two asymptotic ends. Using the covariant phase space formalism, we show that the Hamiltonian charges associated with the two asymptotic regions are finite under appropriate scalar boundary conditions. By performing a double Wick rotation to Euclidean signature, we demonstrate that the conformal class of the resulting metric admits an ambi-Kähler structure.

</details>


### [107] [Exact black hole solutions in Palatini Kalb-Ramond gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05799)
*Lin-Yi She,Rui-Jie Niu,Ke Yang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了Palatini Kalb-Ramond引力中静态球对称黑洞，该理论中Kalb-Ramond场的非零真空期望值自发破缺局域洛伦兹对称性；得到了含正、零、负宇宙常数的精确解；确认了Lorentz破缺参数ℓ₁和ℓ₂可同时非零；利用Iyer-Wald形式计算黑洞质量，并在Λ≤0时验证热力学第一定律与Smarr关系；通过太阳系实验（水星进动等）对组合参数q=ℓ₂/(1−ℓ₁)给出强约束：−7.4×10⁻¹² ≤ q ≤ 3.7×10⁻¹¹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究洛伦兹对称性破缺背景下黑洞的结构与可观测效应，弥补纯度规框架下无法同时容纳两个Lorentz破缺参数的缺陷，并检验该理论与太阳系精密观测的兼容性。

Method: 采用Palatini变分法（度规与无挠联络独立变分），推导修正场方程；求解静态球对称黑洞精确解；应用Iyer-Wald形式计算守恒质量；对Λ=0解实施后牛顿近似，结合水星近日点进动、星光偏折和Shapiro延迟进行参数约束。

Result: 获得含任意Λ的精确黑洞解；证明Palatini框架允许ℓ₁, ℓ₂均非零；导出质量作为热力学焓；太阳系实验将Lorentz破缺效应约束为单一组合参数q，其中水星进动给出最强限制：−7.4×10⁻¹² ≤ q ≤ 3.7×10⁻¹¹。

Conclusion: Palatini Kalb-Ramond引力能自洽描述Lorentz破缺黑洞，其Λ≤0解具有完整黑洞热力学；Λ=0解与现有太阳系观测高度一致，对新物理参数施加了迄今最严苛的直接限制，凸显该理论的可检验性与现象学价值。

Abstract: We investigate static and spherically symmetric black holes in Palatini Kalb-Ramond gravity, where a nonzero vacuum expectation value of the Kalb-Ramond field spontaneously breaks local Lorentz symmetry. Treating the metric and the torsion-free affine connection as independent variables, we derive the modified field equations and obtain an exact family of solutions encompassing positive, vanishing, and negative values of the cosmological constant $Λ$. Unlike the corresponding purely metric formulation, the Palatini theory consistently allows both Lorentz-violating parameters $\ell_1$ and $\ell_2$ to be nonzero. Using the Iyer-Wald formalism, we determine the conserved mass of the black hole solutions. For the $Λ\leq0$ sector, we further verify the first law and the Smarr relation, identifying the conserved mass as the thermodynamic enthalpy. We then confront the $Λ=0$ solution with Mercury's perihelion precession, solar light deflection, and the Shapiro time delay. Once the central mass parameter is calibrated by planetary dynamics, these observables constrain the Lorentz-violating parameters only through $q\equiv\ell_2/(1-\ell_1)$. Among these tests, Mercury's perihelion precession yields the most stringent constraint, $-7.4\times10^{-12} \leq q \leq 3.7\times10^{-11}$.

</details>


### [108] [Gravitational-wave Echoes Beyond the Hubble Time from Quantum Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05886)
*Che-Yu Chen,Naritaka Oshita,Yuki Yokokura*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了无事件视界致密天体模型中的引力波回声，发现由于极强的红移效应，奇宇称引力波被完全困在天体内，回声延迟时间远超宇宙年龄，因此实际不可观测，与当前回声搜索的零结果一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释当前引力波回声搜寻未发现信号的原因，并探讨量子黑洞与经典黑洞的可区分性。

Method: 采用经典广义相对论中各向异性流体模型，分析半经典爱因斯坦方程的非微扰解所描述的无视界致密天体对奇宇称引力波的响应。

Result: 奇宇称引力波不在表面反射，而因指数级强红移被完全束缚于天体内部，导致回声延迟时间远大于宇宙年龄，回声信号实际不可观测。

Conclusion: 该模型下引力波回声无法被探测，支持当前观测的零结果；并指出量子黑洞与经典黑洞可能通过其他观测手段（如吸积盘、光子环等）加以区分。

Abstract: We investigate gravitational-wave echoes in a horizonless compact object model, a non-perturbative solution of the semi-classical Einstein equation that reproduces the entropy-area law from the bulk structure. By employing a model of anisotropic fluids in classical general relativity (GR), we show that odd-parity gravitational waves are not reflected at the object's surface but are trapped inside due to the exponentially strong redshift, and that the echo-delay time extremely exceeds the age of the Universe. Consequently, the echo signals are effectively unobservable, which is consistent with current null results from echo searches. We finally discuss how this quantum black hole and the conventional classical black hole could be distinguished observationally.

</details>


### [109] [Exact Thermodynamics and Two-Horizon Relations of Four Rotating Einstein-Maxwell-Maxwell-Dilaton Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06037)
*Hai Huang,Juhua Chen*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文建立了四维渐近平直Einstein-Maxwell-Maxwell-dilaton理论中Kerr-Newman和Kaluza-Klein种子的四个精确旋转嵌入的完整外视界热力学，验证了第一定律、Smarr关系和标量位移恒等式，并发现了两个正有限面积视界上某些热力学量满足的共同二次关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建更广义引力-电磁-标量耦合理论中黑洞解的完备热力学框架，特别是针对含标量场的旋转带电黑洞。

Method: 利用常系数场映射确定允许的耦合与荷变，推导单值质量函数，在指定正则分支及有限面积极值边界上验证热力学第一定律、Smarr关系与标量位移恒等式，并分析双视界热力学量关系。

Result: 获得单值质量函数；验证了第一定律、Smarr关系和标量位移恒等式（含磁片贡献）；发现两正面积视界上组合量2T±S±与S±Ω±/π满足同一由长程力参数决定的二次关系。

Conclusion: 在无标量势的四维Einstein-Maxwell-Maxwell-dilaton理论中，特定旋转嵌入黑洞具有自洽且结构丰富的外视界热力学，其双视界热力学量呈现普适代数约束。

Abstract: We establish complete outer-horizon thermodynamics for four exact rotating embeddings of Kerr--Newman and Kaluza--Klein seeds in four-dimensional, asymptotically flat Einstein--Maxwell--Maxwell--dilaton theory without a scalar potential. Constant-coefficient field maps determine the allowed couplings and charge variations. We obtain single-valued mass functions and verify the first law, Smarr relation, and scalar-shift identity on the stated regular branches and finite-area extremal boundaries, including magnetic patch contributions where required. For two distinct regular horizons with positive finite areas, the horizon combinations $2T_\pm S_\pm$ and $S_\pmΩ_\pm/π$ satisfy a common quadratic relation with the long-range-force parameter determined by the asymptotic mass, electromagnetic charges, and scalar charge.

</details>


### [110] [Black Hole Horizon Fluctuations from Unruh Quantum Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06223)
*Kashif Ammar Yasir*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过连接完整的外部Unruh Wightman函数与有限带宽的随机Schwarzschild几何重构，研究了霍金场关联如何影响黑洞视界位置和表面引力统计特性，揭示了量子应力涨落对黑洞几何扰动的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平均霍金通量无法刻画量子应力涨落如何扰动黑洞几何，需借助包含真空贡献的场关联信息。

Method: 利用完整Unruh Wightman函数，保留入射真空与出射双频支，计算连通应力协方差并进行角投影前处理，再将单极分量通过密度驱动的径向响应传播；采用共享高斯重构分析 lapse 函数的值与导数对不同几何观测量的影响。

Result: 2200次实现显示局部视界半径和表面引力温度参数的相对宽度分别为0.0185和0.0483，结果在直方图与重构检验中一致；协方差导数解释了这种敏感性差异。

Conclusion: 该构造将量子态输入与引力闭合及空间采样假设分离，提供了可复现、有限分辨率下霍金场关联影响视界与表面引力统计的描述。

Abstract: The mean Hawking flux does not determine how quantum stress fluctuations perturb a black-hole geometry. That information resides in field correlations, including vacuum contributions absent from the outgoing luminosity. Here we connect the complete exterior Unruh Wightman function to a finite-band stochastic reconstruction of Schwarzschild geometry. We retain the incoming vacuum and both outgoing frequency branches, form the connected stress covariance before angular projection, and propagate its monopole through a density-driven radial response. A shared Gaussian reconstruction then resolves how the value and derivative of the lapse control different geometric observables. For the specified covariance and noise amplitude, 2,200 realizations give relative widths of 0.0185 for the local horizon radius and 0.0483 for the surface-gravity temperature parameter, consistently across histograms and reconstruction checks. Covariance derivatives explain this unequal sensitivity. The construction separates the quantum-state input from the gravitational closure and spatial sampling assumptions, providing a reproducible finite-resolution description of how Hawking-field correlations affect horizon positions and surface-gravity statistics.

</details>


### [111] [Scalaron Quasinormal and Quasi-Bound Modes of Schwarzschild Black Holes in the Bondi-Sachs Formulation of Metric $f(R)$ Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06370)
*Bishop Mongwane,Didam G. A. Duniya,Seturumane Tema,Sanele Ziqubu,David F. Mota*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究f(R)引力理论中黑洞微扰的标量自由度（scalaron）的动力学行为，特别是其准正规模和准束缚态谱特性，以检验该理论在强场引力下的可观测效应。

Method: 基于Bondi-Sachs形式的线性化特征方程，导出标量场主方程和等效于广义相对论的张量主方程；采用Leaver连分式法计算频域谱；构建四阶收敛的特征线数值演化方案，并用矩阵 pencil 方法分析时域波形以提取频率和阻尼率。

Result: 标量谱仅依赖于无量纲组合μM；频域与时域方法所得基态准正规模高度一致；首次在同一波形的不同时间窗口中独立提取出准正规模与准束缚态的振荡频率和阻尼率，且无需在有限外边界施加模态约束。

Conclusion: Bondi-Sachs框架适用于f(R)引力中黑洞微扰的严格分析；标量子动力学具有清晰的可预测谱结构；时域特征演化结合矩阵 pencil 方法是提取复杂谱分支的有效新途径。

Abstract: We study scalaron quasinormal modes and quasi-bound states of Schwarzschild black holes directly within the Bondi-Sachs formulation of metric $f(R)$ gravity, combining frequency-domain analyses with characteristic time evolution. Starting from the linearised characteristic equations, we obtain the massive scalar master equation and show that the tensor master equation takes the same form as in general relativity. For the analytic models considered here, the linear scalar spectra depend on the $f(R)$ theory only through the dimensionless combination $μM$. We compute both quasinormal and quasi-bound spectral branches using Leaver's continued-fraction method and compare them with independent literature benchmarks. To complement the frequency domain analysis, we evolve the scalaron perturbation equations using a fourth order convergent characteristic scheme. Applying matrix-pencil stability scans to the resulting waveforms, we independently recover fundamental quasinormal frequencies consistent with the continued-fraction results within the measured extraction-stability scales. Finally, for a selected finite-mass case, we demonstrate the extraction of both oscillation frequencies and damping rates of the fundamental quasinormal mode and quasi-bound state from separate time windows of the same waveform at one extraction radius, without imposing either spectral branch at the finite outer boundary.

</details>


### [112] [Time-domain analysis of scalar perturbations of Kerr--Newman black holes with Chern--Simons and Maxwell couplings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06393)
*Yan-Ming Chen,Hyat Huang,Meng-Yun Lai,De-Cheng Zou,Yun Soo Myung,Ming Zhang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了Einstein-Chern-Simons-Maxwell-scalar理论中Kerr-Newman黑洞周围标量扰动的线性行为，以探讨标量化现象的起始条件；通过超球面Rácz-Tóth演化和稳态轴对称零模计算，确定了标量化阈值，并在不同耦合参数下分析了阈值分支的变化规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Einstein-Chern-Simons-Maxwell-scalar理论中Kerr-Newman黑洞标量化现象的起始条件，即线性标量扰动的不稳定性阈值。

Method: 采用超球面Rácz-Tóth时间演化方法与稳态轴对称零模计算相结合，分析线性标量扰动，并将所得阈值与典型参数点的时间域响应进行对比验证。

Result: 发现当CS耦合参数γ̂=0时，η存在两条不对称的实阈值分支；当γ̂=η时，两分支符号相反、幅值不等；当CS与Maxwell耦合不等时，随自旋或CS耦合增强，两实阈值分支发生合并。

Conclusion: 标量化阈值结构强烈依赖于CS与Maxwell耦合参数的相对大小及黑洞自旋，其分支合并现象揭示了参数空间中不稳定性边界的复杂演化。

Abstract: To study the onset of scalarization, we investigate linear scalar perturbations around Kerr--Newman black holes in the Einstein-Chern-Simons-Maxwell-scalar theory with quadratic Chern--Simons (CS) coupling $γ$ ($\hatγ=γ/M^2$) and Maxwell coupling $η$. For this purpose, we employ hyperboloidal Rácz--Tóth evolution and a stationary axisymmetric zero-mode computation. The resulting thresholds are compared with the time-domain response at representative points. For $\hatγ=0$, we find two asymmetric threshold branches in $η$, while for $\hatγ=η$, the two branches have opposite signs and unequal magnitudes. For unequal CS and Maxwell couplings, the two real threshold branches merge as the spin or CS coupling increases.

</details>


### [113] [Dyonic Simpson Visser black-bounce spacetime surrounded by an exotic Einstein cluster in general relativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06523)
*Pedro Cañate,Joseph Sultana,Demosthenes Kazanas*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了带电（电荷和磁荷）的Simpson-Visser黑跳（SV-BB）时空，作为广义相对论耦合麦克斯韦电磁场与径向压强为零的各向异性流体（爱因斯坦星团）的精确解；该模型实现了正则黑洞时空，并用星团提供维持黑跳喉部开放所需的奇异物质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建一个物理上更自洽的正则黑洞模型，避免奇点，同时解释维持黑跳结构所需的奇异物质来源。

Method: 求解爱因斯坦场方程，源项包含麦克斯韦电磁场与代表爱因斯坦星团的各向异性流体（仅具切向压强、径向压强为零）。

Result: 得到了带电（dyonic）Simpson-Visser黑跳度规的精确解，证明其可由电磁场加爱因斯坦星团共同支持，且时空正则、无奇点。

Conclusion: dyonic SV-BB时空是广义相对论中一个物理上更合理的正则黑洞候选者，其奇异物质可由可观测的致密星团机制提供，增强了黑跳模型的理论可信度与潜在可检验性。

Abstract: In this work, we present the Simpson-Visser black-bounce (SV-BB) spacetime with both electric and magnetic charges (the dyonic SV-BB) as an exact solution of general relativity coupled to Maxwell electrodynamics and an anisotropic fluid with vanishing radial pressure. We assume the anisotropic fluid represents an Einstein cluster, which consists of a dense system of particles moving in circular orbits that generate only tangential pressure and zero radial pressure. We show that the dyonic SV-BB metric, which provides a regular black hole spacetime, can be obtained as an exact solution to the Einstein field equations sourced by a combination of an Einstein cluster (which is responsible for providing the exotic matter required to keep a black-bounce throat open) and an electromagnetic field in the framework of Maxwell electrodynamics.

</details>


### [114] [Cherenkov Bursts are Memory Steps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06528)
*Jann Zosso,Guangzi Xu,Vitor Cardoso*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新视角，将切伦科夫辐射解释为一对干涉的电磁记忆跃迁，揭示了其与端点加速度引发的记忆效应之间的深层联系，并指出阿斯卡良效应可作为实验探测宏观记忆跃迁的新平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解切伦科夫辐射的物理本质，并探索其与电磁记忆效应（一种红外现象）之间的联系，从而为红外物理提供新的实验检验途径。

Method: 通过分析超光速电荷轨迹端点加速度所激发的横向矢量势中的相反记忆阶跃，结合干涉机制和渐近行为分析，解释有限频率辐射及切伦科夫方向上的发散抵消现象。

Result: 发现切伦科夫辐射本质上源于两个干涉的记忆跃迁；当趋近切伦科夫方向时，记忆振幅增大而时间分离减小，最终跃迁重叠并使发散项相消，产生有限辐射脉冲。

Conclusion: 切伦科夫辐射可被统一理解为电磁记忆效应的表现形式，该观点不仅深化了对经典辐射机制的认识，还为在阿斯卡良效应中直接观测宏观记忆跃迁提供了理论依据和实验可能性。

Abstract: We show that the familiar Cherenkov burst from a finite superluminal charge track can be understood as a pair of interfering electromagnetic memory transitions. Endpoint accelerations of the track produce opposite memory steps in the transverse vector potential, such that the corresponding finite-frequency radiation is universally fixed, below the endpoint-acceleration-scale, by the memory amplitude and the time for which the temporary memory offset persists. As the Cherenkov direction is approached, the memory amplitudes grow while their separation in observer time shrinks, until the two vector-potential steps overlap and their divergent contributions cancel into a finite Cherenkov burst. This perspective opens the door to using the Askaryan effect as a setting in which individual macroscopic memory transitions may be resolved, providing a new experimental arena for memory-linked infrared physics.

</details>


### [115] [Puncture Evolution with Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06776)
*Estuti Shukla,Ashwin Suriyanarayanan,David Radice,Romit Maulik,Sebastiano Bernuzzi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了物理信息神经网络（PINNs）在球对称真空爱因斯坦方程求解中的应用，通过设计特定网络结构并最小化包含数据项、里奇张量及移动针尖规范条件的损失函数，成功实现了对数值相对论数据的插值、预测与时空重构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索PINNs在广义相对论数值模拟（特别是黑洞演化）中的潜力，弥补传统数值方法在灵活性、可微性及数据稀缺场景下的不足。

Method: 构建以针尖规范下正则变量（lapse、shift、χ、γ̃rr）为输出的PINN架构；损失函数融合NR数据项、真空爱因斯坦方程（由里奇张量体现）及移动针尖规范条件；在单黑洞演化问题中进行多场景测试：插值、外推、无内边界数据下的内部重构。

Result: PINN能高精度插值NR数据并提供紧凑可微表示；仅用初值和外边界数据即可演化针尖并复现NR主要特征；即使无规范约束和内部数据，也能自主学习坐标系并恢复Schwarzschild时空（验证其到Kerr–Schild坐标的变换）。

Conclusion: PINNs在数值相对论中展现出作为灵活、可微、数据高效建模工具的前景，但也存在不适定逆问题、坐标歧义性等固有局限，需结合物理先验进一步发展。

Abstract: We investigate the application of physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) to solve the vacuum Einstein equations in spherical symmetry. Our neural network architecture is designed to solve the 1+1D moving-puncture evolution, by minimizing a loss that combines a data term, the Ricci tensor of the reconstructed spacetime, and the moving-puncture gauge conditions. It represents the lapse, the shift and the Z4c conformal variables $χ$ and $\widetildeγ_{rr}$, which remain regular at the puncture. We consider the evolution of a single, non-spinning black-hole in the puncture gauge and show that PINNs can interpolate numerical relativity (NR) data, predict its evolution, and reconstruct spacetime in regions where no data is provided. As an interpolant, the network provides a compact, differentiable representation of the NR data. Given only early-time and outer-boundary data, it evolves the puncture forward in time and reproduces the main features of the NR solution. Given only exterior data and no gauge condition, an ill-posed inverse problem, it recovers the interior of a Schwarzschild spacetime in coordinates of its own choosing, which we verify by learning the coordinate transformation to the Kerr--Schild form. Finally, we assess the strengths and limitations of PINNs for NR and outline their prospects for future applications.

</details>


<div id='hep-th'></div>

### [116] [Supplementary Notes on VOA and 2D SCFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03725)
*Taizan Watari*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文整理了二维非全纯共形场论（CFT）的顶点算子代数（VOA）与顶点算子超代数（SVOA）表述，重点补充物理教材中未详述的细节，并系统推广至满足四条件的简单电流扩展类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为作者私人整理非全纯二维CFT材料，以顶点算子代数语言统一表述，并补足物理教材中缺失的微妙细节。

Method: 采用顶点算子代数（VOA）和顶点算子超代数（SVOA）框架，引入两个辅助参数表述Type 0 CFT中的交叉对称性及Ramond/Ramond-Ramond扇区的超算子；提出并验证四条件以刻画简单电流扩展类。

Result: 建立了非全纯Type 0 CFT在SVOA下的交叉对称性与超算子形式化表述；给出了简单电流扩展的四个明确判据，并实现系统性推广。

Conclusion: 该工作为非全纯CFT提供了更严谨、可推广的代数结构基础，有助于深化对Ramond扇区及简单电流扩展的理解与应用。

Abstract: Sections 2--3 of this note were prepared primarily for the author's private use, as a means of organizing the materials in (generally non-holomorphic) conformal field theory (CFT) on 2-dimensions in the language of the vertex operator algebra, and also of collecting subtle details. The choice of topics is intended to supplement introductory physics textbooks on the subject.
  Section 4 deals with (generally non-holomorphic) Type 0 CFTs in the language of vertex operator superalgebra (SVOA). Formulations of crossing symmetry and intertwining superoperators [resp. CFT vertex superoperators] of states in the Ramond (SVOA-twisted) sector [resp. the Ramond--Ramond sector] are presented by using two auxiliary parameters. The formulation allows systematic generalization to the class of simple current extensions characterized by four conditions: (1) the vanishing third cohomology, (2) skew-symmetric bimultiplicative form, (3) fixed-point-free action of simple currents, and (4) one additional condition described in the main text.

</details>


### [117] [Comment on ''Chiral electromagnetic surface waves on Chern-Simons interfaces'' Phys.Rev.D114(2026)L051903 and on arXiv:2606.12513 by N. Kaloper](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03843)
*Igor Bandos,Bohdan Zvorskyi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文指出，麦克斯韦电动力学与平面陈-西蒙斯界面相互作用时，并不存在局域在界面上的平面波解；作者修正了Kaloper先前工作中被遗漏的关键项，并质疑了另一篇文献中所用方法的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重新检验Kaloper等人关于界面上存在局域平面波解的结论，因其对拓扑界面物理和有效理论建模具有重要意义。

Method: 通过严格复核原始推导过程，识别并补全被忽略的方程项；同时分析另一篇文献中所用方法的数学自洽性。

Result: 发现原推导中遗漏了关键项，导致结论错误；修正后证明此类局域平面波解并不存在；且另一方法在逻辑上不自洽。

Conclusion: 麦克斯韦场与平面陈-西蒙斯界面耦合系统中不存在严格局域于界面的平面波解，此前相关结论需修正。

Abstract: We demonstrate that Maxwell electrodynamics interacting with a planar Chern-Simons interface does not have plane wave solutions localized on the interface. We re-examine the derivation of such waves presented by N. Kaloper in [Phys.Rev.D114(2026)L051903] and show that at an intermediate step certain terms were missed in an equation therein. Taking these terms into account changes the final conclusion of [Phys.Rev.D114(2026)L051903]. We also show that the method used in [arXiv:2606.12513 [hep-th]] to reproduce the same result is not consistent.

</details>


### [118] [Complex Worldline Instantons in (Anti-)de Sitter Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03862)
*Alberto Castellano,Jinwei Chu,Eliseu Kloster,Jamie A. P. Law-Smith*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了在二维最大对称背景（含均匀电场）中，带电重粒子的世界线路径积分的复鞍点，发现复化时空与固有时是获得半经典鞍点的必要条件，并揭示了不同几何背景下（AdS、dS、Minkowski）瞬子结构的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强场量子电动力学中非微扰效应（如真空衰变）的微观机制，特别是世界线路径积分框架下复鞍点的物理意义与分类。

Method: 采用世界线路径积分形式，系统分析复鞍点解的结构；复化目标时空与固有时参数；引入拓扑数据（绕数、孤立奇点）刻画瞬子扇区；利用留数定理重构作用量；考察局部解延拓至全局(反)德西特空间的相容性条件。

Result: 1) 复鞍点天然分为实固有时与虚固有时两支，分别主导量子干涉与指数压制；2) 瞬子作用量仅依赖拓扑数据；3) AdS中存在由R|eE|/m决定的亚/极/超极端区分，而dS和Minkowski无此区分；4) 作用量可表为共轭动量留数之和；5) 全局延拓施加额外贡献条件。

Conclusion: 世界线复鞍点具有统一的复家族结构，其物理表现强烈依赖于背景几何的曲率特性；AdS中的临界比揭示了内在尺度竞争，为强场QFT提供了新的拓扑-几何分类视角。

Abstract: We study complex saddle points of the worldline path integral for massive charged particles in two-dimensional maximally symmetric backgrounds threaded by homogeneous electric fields. Within the worldline formalism, we show that the semiclassical saddles generically require complexifying both the target spacetime and the proper-time parameter. The resulting solutions comprise two slices of a single complex family, distinguished by the direction of proper-time evolution. The one evolving in imaginary proper time carries an action that typically controls the exponential suppression of non-perturbative effects, while that evolving in real proper time may encode additional complex phases responsible for quantum interference between saddles. Treating this family as a whole, we give a prescription for constructing and classifying all topologically inequivalent instanton sectors, and we find that the worldline action depends only on their topological data, namely winding numbers around the compact directions and certain isolated punctures. Moreover, in Anti-de Sitter spacetime, the qualitative structure of the solutions is governed by the dimensionless ratio $R |eE|/m$ ($R$ being the curvature radius), which distinguishes sub-extremal, extremal, and super-extremal regimes. By contrast, no analogous distinction occurs in de Sitter---where the instantons are insensitive to this ratio---or in Minkowski spacetime, which lacks a curvature scale altogether. We also recast the action as a sum over residues of the relevant (non-conserved) conjugate momentum, making transparent which analytic data control each contribution. Finally, we discuss the subtleties that arise when solutions built in various local patches are lifted to global (Anti-)de Sitter space. These impose further necessary conditions on which saddles can in principle contribute to the one-loop path integral.

</details>


### [119] [Magnetic Higher-Form Symmetries as Dual Fundamental $\infty$-Groupoids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03865)
*Alonso Perez-Lona*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个高几何框架，用于研究预量子场论中的磁性高形式对称性，通过堆叠目标流形的形状编码磁荷，并利用同伦固定点谱序列等工具分析磁性对称性的结构与性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为理解预量子场论中磁性高形式对称性的数学本质与物理实现，需发展能处理堆叠目标、非有限/非离散数据及高阶拓扑结构的统一几何框架。

Method: 采用高阶几何（higher-geometric）与叠（stacky）方法，结合形状模态（shape modality）、Pontryagin 对偶推广、同伦固定点谱序列及 $E_\infty$-群对象的纤维化定理；对 $U(1)$-高阶胚联络模空间应用因子分解定理。

Result: 证明了两个核心定理：（1）当对偶化叠是 grouplike $E_\infty$-对象时，电性规范理论的磁性对称性可由原理论与规范群的磁性对称性通过纤维化定理刻画；（2）对含联络的 $U(1)$-高阶胚模空间，任意连通目标的磁性对称性背景可非典范地分解为其整同调群的 Pontryagin 对偶，且必为平坦。以 $\mathrm{Pin}^\pm(2)$ 规范理论为例验证了这些结果。

Conclusion: 该框架系统统一了磁性高形式对称性的几何描述与计算方法，揭示了其与电性对称性、规范结构及拓扑局部拉格朗日量之间的深层对偶关系，为后续物理应用（如规范耦合与反常分析）奠定基础。

Abstract: We develop a higher-geometric framework for magnetic higher-form symmetries of pre-quantum field theories. The magnetic (topological) charges of a sigma-model with stacky target are encoded by the shape of the target, equivalently of its stack of fields, which is op-dual to the flat automorphism group controlling electric symmetries. Magnetic symmetry backgrounds are obtained by dualizing these charges against a chosen moduli stack of topological field theories, generalizing Pontryagin duality, and act on the theory by stacking. Cohesive modalities allow for targets with non-finite and non-discrete data. When the dualizing stack is a grouplike $E_\infty$-object, we prove a fibration theorem expressing the magnetic symmetries of an electrically gauged theory in terms of those of the ungauged theory and of the gauge group, computed by a homotopy-fixed-point spectral sequence with an explicit first page. For moduli stacks of higher $U(1)$-gerbes with connection, encoding bulk topological local Lagrangians, we prove a factorization theorem: the magnetic symmetry backgrounds of any connected target split, non-canonically, into Pontryagin duals of its integral homology groups, and are necessarily flat, as a consequence of dualizing discrete charges against a target with connections. We illustrate both theorems with pure $\mathrm{Pin}^\pm(2)$ gauge theory: $O(2)$ carries invertible magnetic $\mathbb{Z}_2$ symmetries in form degrees $d-3$, $d-4$ and $d-5$ that are absent for $\mathrm{Pin}^-(2)$, the first two distinguished by a single differential of the spectral sequence. Gauging and physical applications are developed in upcoming work.

</details>


### [120] [SU($N$) QCD, four-manifolds and Segre numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03874)
*Elias Furrer,Ranveer Kumar Singh*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了四维紧致流形X上拓扑扭曲的N=2超对称QCD（SU(N)规范群）的相关函数，推导出含味对称性背景通量耦合的库仑分支积分公式；发现当X为简单型且b2+>1时，相关函数可表示为有限个强耦合奇点上的求和，并与N=1真空及瞬子分支贡献相关；提出SU(N) QCD瞬子贡献对应于代数曲面上半稳定层模空间的虚拟Segre数生成函数的猜想，并建立了Segre数普适级数与库仑分支耦合之间的精确字典；发展了涉及Siegel模形式、Z_N对称性、电荷共轭对称性、Seiberg-Witten曲线分解及爆炸公式的高秩计算技术；对纯SU(N)情形复现了Mariño-Moore结果，对SU(3)情形给出了Nf=1~5下全部13个库仑耦合的闭式解，并与Göttsche-Kool的虚拟秩3 Segre数结果精确吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解N=2超对称QCD在四维紧致流形上的拓扑相关函数，特别是其与代数几何中虚拟Segre数等几何不变量之间的深刻联系，并建立物理参数（如库仑分支耦合）与数学不变量（如Segre数）之间的精确对应关系。

Method: 基于拓扑扭曲的N=2超对称QCD理论，推导适用于任意N的库仑分支积分表达式，引入味对称性背景通量耦合；利用四维流形X的拓扑性质（如简单型、b2+>1）简化相关函数为有限强耦合奇点求和；结合N=1破缺极限识别瞬子分支贡献；发展高秩计算技术（包括Siegel模形式、Z_N对称性、电荷共轭、Seiberg-Witten曲线分解、爆炸公式）以精确计算库仑分支耦合；构建Segre数普适级数与库仑耦合间的字典。

Result: 得到任意N下含背景通量的库仑分支积分；证明相关函数由有限强耦合奇点（含N=1真空）贡献；提出并验证SU(N) QCD瞬子贡献等于虚拟Segre数生成函数的猜想；建立Segre数普适级数与库仑耦合的精确对应；对纯SU(N)复现Donaldson不变量；对SU(3) QCD给出Nf=1~5下全部13个库仑耦合的闭式解，并与Göttsche-Kool的虚拟秩3 Segre数结果完全一致。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了N=2超对称QCD的拓扑相关函数与代数几何中虚拟Segre数之间的深刻统一性，通过发展新的高秩数学物理工具，不仅严格验证了多个特例下的猜想，还建立了普适的物理-几何对应字典，为理解四维规范理论、低维拓扑不变量与代数几何不变量之间的深层联系提供了关键桥梁。

Abstract: We study correlation functions of topologically twisted $\mathcal N=2$ supersymmetric QCD with gauge group $\mathrm{SU}(N)$ on a compact four-manifold $X$. We derive the Coulomb branch integral for all $N$, including new couplings to background fluxes for the flavor symmetry. For $X$ of simple type with $b_2^+>1$, the correlators become sums over a finite number of strong-coupling singularities on the Coulomb branch, and can be expressed using a number of Coulomb branch couplings. The sum contains the $\mathcal N=1$ vacua, which become the supersymmetric vacua when the $\mathcal N=2$ theory is broken to $\mathcal N=1$ by an adjoint mass deformation, and whose contribution we attribute to the instanton branch. We focus on asymptotically free $\mathrm{SU}(N)$ QCD with $N_f<2N$ equal mass hypermultiplets, and conjecture that its instanton contribution to topological correlators agrees with the generating function of virtual Segre numbers of rank $N$, which are intersection numbers on the moduli space of semistable sheaves on an algebraic surface. We present a precise dictionary for all $N$ between universal series for Segre numbers and Coulomb branch couplings at the $\mathcal N=1$ vacua. The calculation of these couplings requires new higher-rank techniques, involving Siegel modular forms, an exact $\mathbb Z_N$ symmetry, charge conjugation symmetry, factorisation of the Seiberg--Witten curve, and the blowup formula. For pure $\mathrm{SU}(N)$, our result agrees with earlier work by Mariño and Moore, and the twisted correlators give the $\mathrm{SU}(N)$ Donaldson invariants of $X$. For $\mathrm{SU}(3)$ QCD, we determine closed-form expressions for all 13 Coulomb branch couplings for each $N_f=1,\dots, 5$, and find precise agreement with results of Göttsche and Kool for virtual rank 3 Segre numbers.

</details>


### [121] [Quantum covariant bit threads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03929)
*Matthew Headrick,Sreeman Reddy Kasireddy,Andrew Rolph*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了量子协变比特线（quantum covariant bit threads）这一新方法，作为量子极值面（QES）公式的等价替代，用极小/极大体流而非极值面来刻画全息纠缠熵，并揭示了QES作为‘量子瓶颈’的物理图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为在包含动力学和体量子修正（如黑洞蒸发）的情形下，提供比标准QES公式更直观、更具几何与信息流意义的全息纠缠熵表述方式。

Method: 构建两类量子协变比特线：类空V流和类时U流；允许流线在体内部起止，其端点分布由体纠缠熵调控；将QES解释为限制流的‘量子瓶颈’，部分线穿过（对应广义熵的面积项），部分线跃过（对应熵项）。

Result: 得到了与QES公式严格等价的比特线表述；为黑洞蒸发、岛屿（islands）和婴儿宇宙等关键现象提供了新的几何与信息流视角。

Conclusion: 量子协变比特线是QES公式的有力补充，不仅形式等价，而且增强了对量子引力中纠缠结构的物理理解与可视化能力。

Abstract: Entanglement entropy plays a central role in holography. The quantum extremal surface (QES) formula is the standard tool in settings where both dynamics and bulk quantum corrections are important, such as in black hole evaporation. We derive and explore QES-equivalent prescriptions --- quantum covariant bit threads --- that formulate holographic entanglement entropy in terms of minimal or maximal bulk flows rather than extremal surfaces. Like classical covariant bit threads, our prescriptions come in two types: (typically) spacelike V-flows, and timelike U-flows. To account for bulk quantum corrections, the flow lines can start and end at points in the bulk, with the distribution of endpoints controlled by bulk entanglement entropies. In our bit thread language, the QES is a quantum bottleneck to the flow, with some threads passing through it (accounting for the area term in the generalized entropy) and others jumping across it (accounting for the entropy term). Our prescriptions provide a complementary perspective to the QES formula and offer new insights on important phenomena such as black hole evaporation, islands, and baby universes.

</details>


### [122] [Exact effective central charges for a family of 3d superconformal field theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03950)
*Griffen Adams*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出一个精确公式，用于计算通过3d-3d对应与三维流形上的陈-西蒙斯理论相关的三维超共形场论的有效中心荷ceff，并通过渐近重求和方法验证了该公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找能准确描述三维超共形场论中受保护态增长行为的有效中心荷ceff的精确表达式。

Method: 基于3d-3d对应关系，将三维超共形场论与三维流形上的陈-西蒙斯理论联系起来，并利用模形式空间维数推导ceff的公式；通过resurgent continuation（渐近重求和）进行验证。

Result: 得到一个以特定模形式空间维数表示的有效中心荷ceff的精确公式，并成功复现已知结果。

Conclusion: 该公式为理解相关超共formal场论的受保护谱结构提供了新工具，并强化了3d-3d对应在精确计算中的作用。

Abstract: We conjecture an exact formula for the effective central charge, ceff, of a family of three- dimensional superconformal field theories related to Chern-Simons theory on a three-manifold by the 3d-3d correspondence. The formula determines ceff , which governs the growth of a protected sector of states in the conformal theory, in terms of the dimension of a particular space of modular forms. We confirm our approach by checking against known results computed through resurgent continuation.

</details>


### [123] [Wilson line defect indices for M2-brane SCFTs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04065)
*Hirotaka Hayashi,Tomoki Nosaka,Tadashi Okazaki*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了三维N≥4超对称规范理论中的线缺陷库仑和希格斯指标，提出了无陈-西蒙斯项理论中这两种极限下线缺陷维滕指标的一般公式，并发现了M2膜理论中威尔逊线缺陷库仑指标的强大代数递归关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索三维超对称规范理论中线缺陷的物理性质及其在不同真空分支下的行为，特别是其指标的整数性及可计算性。

Method: 通过定义归一化的线缺陷指标（即带装饰缺陷指标与普通指标的比值），并在单位R荷 fugacity 极限下分析其整数值——线缺陷维滕指标；推导库仑与希格斯极限下的通用公式，并建立适用于多种ADHM型与ABJ(M)理论的代数递归关系。

Result: 得到了无陈-西蒙斯项理论中库仑与希格斯极限下线缺陷维滕指标的通用公式；证明二者均计数对应分支上残余规范群不变的态；发现并验证了适用于U(N)、USp(2N)、SO(2N)、SO(2N+1) ADHM型及ABJ(M)理论的威尔逊线缺陷库仑指标递归关系。

Conclusion: 线缺陷维滕指标具有清晰的物理含义（残余对称性下的不变态计数），且其结构受强代数约束，可通过低秩数据递归确定高秩指标，为三维超共形场论中缺陷算符的研究提供了系统性工具。

Abstract: We investigate line defect Coulomb and Higgs indices in 3d $\mathcal{N}\ge 4$ supersymmetric gauge theories. The normalized line defect indices defined by ratios of the decorated defect indices to the ordinary indices yield well-defined integer values, the line defect Witten indices in the unit R-charge fugacity limit. We propose general formulae for these Witten indices in both Coulomb and Higgs limits for the theories without Chern-Simons terms, and we show that both count the states invariant under the residual gauge group left unbroken at a generic point of the branch, which we read off from the vacuum equations. Furthermore, we find powerful algebraic recursion relations for Wilson line defect Coulomb indices in the 3d SCFTs describing M2-branes, including ADHM-type theories and ABJ(M) theories. These relations imply that higher rank line defect indices are recursively determined by lower rank data across $U(N)$, $USp(2N)$, $SO(2N)$ and $SO(2N+1)$ ADHM-type theories and ABJ(M) theories.

</details>


### [124] [Anomaly-free six-dimensional $Sp(1)_R$-gauged supergravities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04131)
*Katrin Becker,Ergin Sezgin,Qi You*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了六维N=(1,0)超引力理论中由局域反常相消和弦电荷格点嵌入所导致的反常约束，严格限定了矢量多重态数目n_V只能取12或96，并系统分类了满足条件的规范群与超多重态谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索六维N=(1,0)超引力中由反常相消和弦论一致性（如电荷格点）所施加的强约束，以限制可能的低能有效理论。

Method: 基于局域引力、规范及混合反常的抵消条件，并结合反常系数在幺模秩二弦电荷格点中的嵌入要求，推导n_V的可能取值；进一步对不同规范代数分支（半单、可约）进行谱组装与枚举计算。

Result: 得出n_V仅可为12或96；n_V=96时，在排除A1、A2因子的半单非R对称规范代数中无不含drone的解；n_V=12时半单分支必为A1^3，得到4337331个一致模型（分属102类反常向量）；对A2×U(1)×Sp(1)_R可约分支，找到178538个解（分属21组反常向量）。

Conclusion: 六维N=(1,0)超引力中反常与弦格点约束高度协同，极大限制了可能的规范结构与物质谱，凸显了量子引力对低能物理的深刻约束。

Abstract: We study anomaly constraints in six-dimensional $N=(1,0)$ supergravity with gauged $Sp(1)_R$ symmetry, one tensor multiplet, $n_V$ vector multiplets and $n_H$ hypermultiplets. Imposing cancellation of local gravitational, gauge and mixed anomalies, together with the embedding of the anomaly coefficients in a unimodular rank-two string charge lattice, the number of vector multiplets is sharply constrained: the only allowed positive values are $n_V=12$ and $n_V=96$. For a neutral abelian factor, or drone, the anomaly equations force the corresponding row and column of the abelian gauge-kinetic matrix to vanish. Such a factor therefore cannot be retained as an ordinary propagating vector multiplet. For $n_V=96$, within the class of semisimple non-$R$ gauge algebras containing no $A_1$ or $A_2$ factor, the exhaustive spectra-assembly calculation yields no drone-free spectrum. For $n_V=12$, the semisimple non-$R$ branch is necessarily $A_1^3$. In the doublet/adjoint truncation described in the text, we find $4{,}337{,}331$ consistent models, classified by $102$ anomaly-vector classes. We also consider the reductive branch $A_2\times U(1)\times Sp(1)_R$. For $A_2$ representations in $\{\mathbf 1,\mathbf 3,\overline{\mathbf 3}, \mathbf 6,\overline{\mathbf 6},\mathbf 8\}$ and primitive $U(1)$ charge assignments with $|q|\le 2$, we find $178{,}538$ solutions, classified by $21$ sets of anomaly vectors.

</details>


### [125] [Higher-form Symmetries of Linearized Gravity in Maximally Symmetric Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04218)
*Natsuya Kido*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将最大对称时空中的引力场表述为规范理论，并通过牛顿引力常数的级数展开，研究线性化引力场的高形式对称性；利用基于Cartan几何的MacDowell-Mansouri形式，证明了闵可夫斯基时空中线性引力存在1-形式对称性，并推广至小宇宙常数下的dS和AdS时空，给出其近似1-形式对称性及其对宇宙常数的显式依赖关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将引力视为规范理论并系统研究其高形式对称性，尤其在不同最大对称背景（如dS、AdS）下探索对称性的普适性与变形行为。

Method: 采用MacDowell-Mansouri规范理论框架（基于Cartan几何），对线性化引力场按牛顿常数作微扰展开，分析其守恒荷与高形式对称结构。

Result: 证实闵可夫斯基时空线性引力中存在严格1-形式对称性；在小宇宙常数的dS和AdS时空中，存在依赖于宇宙常数的近似1-形式对称性。

Conclusion: 引力在规范理论视角下展现出非平凡的高形式对称性，该对称性在弯曲最大对称背景中以宇宙常数为参数连续形变，揭示了引力与拓扑/全局结构的深层联系。

Abstract: We formulate the gravitational field on maximally symmetric spacetimes as a gauge theory, and investigate higher-form symmetries of the linearized gravitational field via a series expansion in Newton's gravitational constant. To treat gravity as a gauge theory, the MacDowell-Mansouri formulation based on Cartan geometry is utilized. We show the existence of a 1-form symmetry in linearized gravity on Minkowski spacetime. Furthermore, for other maximally symmetric spacetimes (de Sitter and anti-de Sitter spacetimes) with a small cosmological constant, we demonstrate the existence of an approximate 1-form symmetry in the corresponding linearized gravity, detailing its explicit dependence on the cosmological constant.

</details>


### [126] [Wald entropy and Wen-Zee type gauge-curvature coupling in 2+1-dimensional gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04476)
*Aryan Bhatia,Giandomenico Palumbo,Suraj S. Hegde*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了Wen-Zee项在洛伦兹协变框架下的推广，将其应用于黑洞时空，并计算其对事件视界熵的贡献；该耦合自然出现在基于Maxwell代数的三维Chern-Simons引力中，其Wald熵贡献表现为一种视界上的‘提升荷’，可解释为沿分叉圆切向投影的额外规范场的环路积分；该贡献即使在壳上混合项为零时仍可非零，并在平坦宇宙解和AdS-Lorentz黑洞上进行了具体计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将量子霍尔流体中描述曲率-电荷耦合的Wen-Zee项推广到相对论性引力背景（特别是黑洞时空），以探索其在引力热力学（尤其是视界熵）中的物理意义。

Method: 基于Maxwell代数的三维Chern-Simons引力理论构建洛伦兹协变推广的Wen-Zee型耦合；采用Wald熵公式计算该耦合对黑洞视界熵的贡献；将结果诠释为视界上的几何boost电荷，并借助Abelian全息解释其拓扑性质；在平坦宇宙解和AdS-Lorentz黑洞精确解上进行具体计算。

Result: 该推广Wen-Zee耦合对Wald熵的贡献是一个视界boost电荷，由沿分叉圆切向投影的额外规范场的环路积分给出；该电荷可在壳上混合项消失时仍非零；在特定条件下（投影方向协变常数）该积分具有Abelian全息解释；在AdS-Lorentz黑洞上得到明确非零熵修正。

Conclusion: Wen-Zee型几何响应机制不仅存在于凝聚态系统，在引力理论中亦有深刻对应——其推广形式为黑洞视界熵提供新的拓扑/几何起源，架起了量子霍尔物理与引力热力学之间的桥梁。

Abstract: The Wen-Zee term is a mixed gauge-curvature coupling in the topological Chern-Simons description of a quantum Hall fluid, encoding the electric charge bound to spatial curvature. We investigate a Lorentz-covariant generalisation of this coupling, expanding its scope to black-hole spacetimes and determine its contribution to horizon entropy. Such a coupling arises naturally in three-dimensional Chern-Simons gravity based on the Maxwell algebra. We show that its Wald entropy contribution is a horizon boost charge, given by a loop integral of the additional gauge field projected along the tangent to the bifurcation circle. This contribution can be nonzero even when the mixed term vanishes on shell. We show that the integral admits an Abelian holonomy interpretation when the projection direction is covariantly constant. We evaluate the charge on a flat cosmological solution and, using smooth horizon fields and a specified thermal ensemble, on an AdS-Lorentz black hole. These results connect the geometric response familiar from quantum Hall physics to gravitational horizon thermodynamics.

</details>


### [127] [Majorana bilinears, compact modular seeds and the approach to the Tsirelson bound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04729)
*M. S. Guimaraes,I. Roditi,S. P. Sorella*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了1+1维自由大质量马约拉纳场真空中的Bell-CHSH不等式，使用右/左Rindler楔形区域中局域化的偶次双线性算符作为二分观测算符；通过Tomita-Takesaki算符构造测试函数，发现对正模频率种子，单粒子重叠与模权重无关，并给出Bell参数的闭式解；当种子为单紧支凸起的相位旋转时，双线性算符在每个楔形中生成Pauli代数副本，并精确计算了真空在此两副本上的关联矩阵；最优设置下违反经典界限的程度显著优于平面设置；同时证明楔形真空无法达到Tsirelson界，趋近该界要求Bell矢量的模谱权重集中在模算符的谱点λ=1处，从而精确刻画了自由马约拉纳场偶部实现Summers-Werner机制的方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究自由大质量Majorana场真空在相对论性加速参考系（Rindler楔形）中量子非定域性的极限表现，特别是Bell-CHSH不等式的违背程度及其与代数量子场论中模理论（modular theory）的深层联系，旨在精确理解Summers-Werner机制在具体可解模型中的实现。

Method: 基于代数量子场论框架，选取Rindler右/左楔形区域中由偶次双线性 smeared 场构成的 dichotomic 观测算符；利用右楔形的Tomita-Takesaki算符及其伴随乘以i（体现扭曲对偶性）构造测试函数；针对正模频率种子，解析计算单粒子重叠与Bell参数；对相位旋转紧支种子情形，显式构建Pauli代数副本并精确求解真空限制下的2×2关联矩阵；分析Bell参数极值及Tsirelson界可达性，结合模算符谱性质进行严格论证。

Result: 1) 对正模频率种子，Bell参数获得闭式解；2) 相位旋转紧支种子下，得到精确关联矩阵，最优Bell参数突破经典界限的幅度超过平面设置对应值的两倍；3) 严格证明楔形真空无法达到Tsirelson界；4) 趋近Tsirelson界必然导致Bell矢量模谱权重在λ=1处集中。

Conclusion: 自由Majorana场的偶部在Rindler真空下能显著违背Bell-CHSH不等式，其最大违背程度受模算符谱结构严格约束；Tsirelson界不可达性及λ=1谱点的临界作用，为Summers-Werner机制——即局部代数间固有非经典关联源于模理论结构——提供了具体、严格的实现范例与定量刻画。

Abstract: In this work we study the Bell-CHSH inequality in the vacuum of the free massive Majorana field in $1+1$ dimensions, taking as the dichotomic observables of the two parties even bilinears of smeared fields localized in the right and in the left Rindler wedges. The test functions are generated from seeds in the modular-frequency variable through the Tomita-Takesaki operator of the right wedge and, on the side of the left wedge, through its adjoint multiplied by $i$, which accounts for twisted duality. For seeds supported at positive modular frequency the one-particle overlaps between the two parties do not depend on the modular weights, and we obtain the Bell parameter in closed form. When the seeds are phase rotations of a single compact bump, the bilinears generate a copy of the Pauli algebra in each wedge, and we compute exactly the correlation matrix of the vacuum restricted to the two copies. The optimal settings violate the classical bound up to spectral widths more than twice as large as those reached with planar settings. We also show that the Tsirelson bound is never attained in the wedge vacuum for any choice of dichotomic observables, and that approaching it forces the modular spectral weight of the Bell vectors to concentrate at the spectral point $λ=1$ of the modular operator. This establishes in a precise way how the even sector of the free Majorana field realizes the Summers-Werner mechanism.

</details>


### [128] [An Observer in de Sitter Space, and Rereading Everett](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04804)
*Edward Witten*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了Hugh Everett的经典文章，从生活在德西特空间中的观察者角度探讨了德西特空间的熵，强调在宇宙学中观察者本身是被观测宇宙的一部分这一事实的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强调在宇宙学研究中，观察者并非外部独立存在，而是宇宙系统的一部分，这对理解如德西特空间熵等特定问题至关重要。

Method: 基于Hugh Everett的理论框架，结合对德西特空间的物理分析，从内部观察者视角出发进行概念性与理论性探讨。

Result: 阐明了德西特空间熵的理解必须考虑观察者内嵌于宇宙之中的特性，为相关宇宙学问题提供了新视角。

Conclusion: 观察者的内在性是理解某些宇宙学问题（如德西特熵）的关键前提，忽略这一点可能导致概念上的偏差。

Abstract: After re-examining a classic article by Hugh Everett, I will discuss the entropy of de Sitter space from the standpoint of an observer living in the space. This provides a nice example of the importance -- for certain questions -- of taking seriously the fact that in cosmology any observer is really part of the universe that is being observed. (This article has appeared in a special issue of International Journal of Modern Physics A in honor of Gerard 't Hooft.

</details>


### [129] [Scalar potential bounds on one-parameter Calabi-Yau threefolds with fluxes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04858)
*Nana Geraldine Cabo Bizet,José de J. Pérez Lona*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了IIB型弦理论在一参数Calabi-Yau三维流形紧化下的多场通量势中 refined de Sitter 猜想（RdSC）、Trans-Planckian Censorship Conjecture（TCC）及物种尺度Λ_s 的行为，通过精确周期函数分析包括镜像五次曲面在内的模型，在统计和解析层面验证了RdSC与TCC约束，并指出TCC导出的紧化体积下界是主导约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验弦理论中de Sitter真空的存在性及其与量子引力猜想（如RdSC、TCC）的相容性，尤其在可精确求解的一参数Calabi-Yau模型中深入探究通量势的临界点性质与多场动力学。

Method: 基于Picard-Fuchs方程获得精确周期，构建h^{2,1}=1情形下的完整通量势；对镜像五次曲面进行200组通量扫描；结合no-scale结构、SL(2)-协变形式与数值模拟，分析临界点稳定性、势梯度、TCC约束及物种尺度场范围限制。

Result: 96%通量构型存在不稳定极值点；RdSC第一条件普遍满足（梯度恒为√6）；TCC要求势梯度趋近2（大复结构）、发散（锥形点）或恰为√2（axio-dilaton方向）；导出紧化体积下界v > v₀ ∼ e^N；TCC比物种尺度约束V ≲ 3Λ_s²更严格。

Conclusion: 在一参数IIB弦紧化中，TCC提供了比RdSC和物种尺度更强的模型约束，且其导出的体积下界是决定de Sitter解可行性的关键物理限制。

Abstract: We study the refined de Sitter conjecture (RdSC), the Trans-Planckian Censorship Conjecture (TCC) and the species scale $Λ_s$ in the multifield flux potential of type IIB string theory compactified on one-parameter Calabi--Yau threefolds. The scalar potential depends on the axio-dilaton and on the single complex-structure modulus, and is no-scale along the Kähler directions. Restricting to $h^{2,1}=1$ allows us to work with the exact periods obtained from the Picard--Fuchs equations rather than with their large-complex-structure expansion, and therefore to probe the conifold region. For the mirror quintic, $(h^{2,1},h^{1,1})=(1,101)$, we scan 200 flux configurations and find unstable extrema in $96\%$ of the cases, with $c'=0.18$. Once the Kähler runaway is taken into account, the no-scale structure fixes the logarithmic slope of the potential at every critical point to the universal value $\sqrt6$, so that the first RdSC condition is satisfied throughout. Statistically, the complex structure is the heavier sector and the axio-dilaton dominates the unstable directions. For the TCC we obtain analytic results valid for the whole class of one-parameter models: the slope approaches the universal value $2$ at large complex structure, diverges at the conifold, and equals exactly $\sqrt2$ in the axio-dilaton directions, where the leading potential takes a single $SL(2)$-covariant form, as the multifield evolutions confirm numerically. The TCC further translates into a lower bound on the compactification volume, $v>v_0\sim e^{N}$, controlled by the number of e-folds. Finally, the field displacements along our trajectories are compatible with the species-scale field-range bound, and restoring the volume dependence of $Λ_s$ shows that $V\lesssim3Λ_s^2$ is implied by the TCC volume bound, which is thus the genuine constraint on these models.

</details>


### [130] [The Phenomenology of the Dark Dimension and Non-Supersymmetric Strings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04909)
*Cumrun Vafa*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文探讨了Swampland原理与暗能量小值Λ结合所引出的'暗维度'场景，提出一个微米尺度的额外维度，并预测了包括KK引力子暗物质、中微子质量与Λ^{1/4}关联、QCD轴子衰变常数f_a～Λ^{1/12}、电弱等级问题与Λ^{1/6}联系等现象；同时该模型实现了演化的暗区，其维度演化将暗能量变化与暗物质质量变化耦合，拟合DESI DR2等观测数据且避免幻影穿越；最后综述了非超对称弦真空的最新进展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于Swampland原理与观测到的极小暗能量值Λ，探索理论一致性与现象学可检验性的统一框架，特别是解释暗能量、暗物质、粒子物理等级问题之间的深层联系。

Method: 结合Swampland约束、5维引力理论（含额外微米尺度维度）、KK紧致化、B-L规范对称性、模空间动力学分析及宇宙学参数拟合（DESI DR2+CMB+超新星），并关联非超对称弦论构造（如非几何通量紧致化、Scherk-Schwarz紧致化）。

Result: 预言了微米级暗维度及其多重可观测后果：KK暗物质、中微子质量标度、轴子衰变常数、电弱/大统一能标关系；成功拟合当前宇宙学数据而无需幻影暗能量；指出暗能量场位于模空间内部靠近不稳定临界点；支持存在由对称性保护的孤立非超对称弦真空。

Conclusion: 暗维度为连接量子引力约束（Swampland）、宇宙学观测与粒子物理标准模型扩展提供了自洽框架；它不仅自然导出多个关键能标关系，还为非超对称弦真空的存在与稳定性提供了现象学动机和理论支撑。

Abstract: Swampland principles, combined with the smallness of dark energy $Λ$, lead to the dark dimension scenario, with one extra dimension of micron size. We review this scenario and its predictions: KK graviton dark matter (or the KK tower of the quintessence field into which the gravitons convert), neutrino masses tied to $Λ^{1/4}$ via a bulk $B-L$ gauge symmetry, QCD axion decay constant $f_a\sim Λ^{1/12}\sim 10^9-10^{10}$ GeV at the 5d Planck scale $M_5$, connecting electroweak hierarchy to $Λ^{1/6}$, and a relation between the GUT and weak scales with $M_5$ as the geometric mean. The dark dimension also provides a concrete realization of an evolving dark sector: the evolution of its diameter correlates the variation of dark energy with that of the dark matter mass, fitting the recent DESI DR2 (+CMB+supernovae) data while avoiding the phantom crossing that the $w_0w_a$CDM parametrization would suggest---the apparent phantom behavior instead arises from the evolving dark matter mass. The fits suggest that the dark energy field sits in the interior of moduli space, near an unstable critical point. Motivated by this, we review recent progress on non-supersymmetric string vacua: isolated critical points protected by duality symmetries, in non-geometric flux compactifications and in Scherk--Schwarz compactifications of M-theory, and an emerging duality web for non-supersymmetric strings. This note is based on two talks given by the author at String Phenomenology 2026 (Paris) and Strings 2026 (Shanghai).

</details>


### [131] [$BF$ effective action topological implications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04962)
*Erika Amador-Ramos,Aldo Martínez-Merino,Roberto Santos-Silva*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了7维拓扑BF理论在4维约化下的有效作用量，探讨了约化后理论的拓扑性质，并在闵可夫斯基和史瓦西时空下得到了运动方程的精确解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解高维拓扑场论在低维约化下的行为及其物理与拓扑含义。

Method: 对7维拓扑BF理论进行4维约化，分析其有效作用量、拓扑性质及场方程，并求解运动方程。

Result: 推导出约化后理论的场方程，并在闵可夫斯基和史瓦西时空中获得其精确解。

Conclusion: 4维约化后的BF理论保持了丰富的拓扑结构，且其运动方程具有可观测时空背景下的解析解，有助于连接拓扑场论与引力物理。

Abstract: We investigate the effective action of a 7-dimensional topological $BF$ theory under a 4-dimensional reduction. We discuss the topological implications of the resulting theory and analyze the corresponding field equations. In particular, we derive exact solutions to these equations of motion in both Minkowski and Schwarzschild spacetimes.

</details>


### [132] [From Critical Zero Modes to Interior Dynamics in Holographic Superconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05208)
*Yan Liu,Hong-Da Lyu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了全息超导体的临界行为如何控制带电黑洞内部几何结构，发现临界零模在Cauchy视界附近引发对数振荡，导致内部临界极限非均匀；该零模的透射幅度与局部Cauchy视界量共同决定Einstein–Rosen坍缩宽度和Josephson相位，并进一步影响Kasner指数振荡及标度律；数值验证涵盖普通与三临界情形；而低温行为则由零温几何的红外完成主导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解全息超导相变临界点附近带电黑洞内部时空结构的演化规律，特别是临界行为如何影响黑洞内部长期动力学（如Cauchy视界稳定性、Kasner振荡等）。

Method: 结合解析渐近分析（聚焦临界零模在Cauchy视界附近的对数振荡行为、透射特性）与全非线性数值模拟（覆盖普通及三临界超导模型），提取临界标度律（如Δz_ER ∝ t^{1/r}, Θ_J ∝ t^{-1/r}）及其对内部几何（Einstein-Rosen坍缩、Josephson相位、Kasner指数振荡）的影响机制。

Result: 发现临界零模透射幅度决定Einstein–Rosen坍缩宽度Δz_ER和Josephson相位Θ_J，二者满足Δz_ER ∝ t^{1/r}、Θ_J ∝ t^{-1/r}；该相位传递至首段Kasner演化，导致Kasner指数振荡及标度律t_n ∼ n^{-r}；所有预测均被全非线性数值解定量证实；同时指出低温行为由红外终点而非临界模式主导。

Conclusion: 黑洞内部近临界动力学由临界零模的传输性质主导，体现为普适标度律与振荡结构；而极低温下则过渡到由红外几何决定的定态行为；这揭示了全息超导相变中时空奇点演化与量子临界性的深刻联系。

Abstract: We study how the critical behavior of holographic superconductors controls the interior geometry of charged black holes. Although the exterior condensate vanishes at criticality, the critical zero mode develops logarithmic oscillations near the Reissner--Nordström Cauchy horizon, rendering the interior critical limit nonuniform. We show that the transmitted critical-mode amplitude, together with local Cauchy-horizon quantities, fixes both the leading Einstein--Rosen collapse $Δz_{\rm ER}$ width and the phase $Θ_J$ in the subsequent Josephson regime. If the condensate obeys $t\equiv1-T/T_c\proptoε^{2r}$, then $Δz_{\rm ER}\propto t^{1/r}$ and $Θ_J\propto t^{-1/r}$. This Josephson phase is transferred to the first Kasner epoch, producing oscillatory Kasner exponents and an accumulation law $t_n\sim n^{-r}$ for successive zeros of the scalar velocity. The critical zero mode fixes the leading collapse and phase coefficients, which are quantitatively confirmed by fully nonlinear solutions in both the ordinary and tricritical cases. At low temperature, the interior instead depends on the infrared completion of the zero-temperature geometry. Thus near-critical scaling is governed by critical-mode transmission, whereas the low-temperature behavior is controlled by the infrared endpoint.

</details>


### [133] [Inflationary magnetogenesis in Proca electrodynamics with Schwinger particle production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05406)
*Mattia Dubbini,Orlando Luongo*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了暴胀磁生成过程中带有质量的矢量场，考虑了通过Schwinger效应产生的带电标量粒子及其对背景电能密度的反作用。结果表明，Schwinger效应在暴胀末期显著降低电能密度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解暴胀时期磁场的起源及带电粒子产生对电磁背景演化的影响，尤其是Schwinger效应的反作用是否改变磁生成机制的有效性。

Method: 对带质量矢量场进行量子化，分别求解横模与纵模运动方程并计算能量动量张量；引入Schwinger效应的反作用，将电能密度视为经典背景，磁与纵模作为量子场处理；采用最小耦合的复标量场计算Schwinger粒子产率。

Result: 纵向能量密度远小于背景电能密度；电能密度主导磁场贡献；Schwinger效应仅显著影响电能密度，并在暴胀末期使其急剧下降；零耦合极限下结果与全量子电磁描述一致。

Conclusion: Schwinger效应不可忽略，它通过耗散电能密度显著抑制暴胀磁生成过程，尤其在暴胀后期；该效应需被纳入暴胀磁生成自洽模型中。

Abstract: We investigate inflationary magnetogenesis with massive vector fields, including charged scalar particle production via the Schwinger effect and its backreaction on the background electric energy density. Initially neglecting the Schwinger effect, we quantize the vector field and solve the equations of motion for transverse and longitudinal modes, computing the energy momentum tensor. We then incorporate the backreaction of the electric energy density and show that the longitudinal energy density is subleading compared to the electric background. Since the electric energy density dominates the magnetic contribution, we can treat the former separately from the perturbative contributions. We adopt this approach when studying particle production via the Schwinger effect, assuming that its backreaction acts only on the background electric energy density. The latter is computed using a classical background electromagnetic field minimally coupled to a quantum complex scalar field. The magnetic and longitudinal components are instead described through quantum fields. In this framework, the Schwinger production affects only the electric energy density, as long as it remains dominant compared to the other components. As a consistency check, we verify that for a null coupling constant we recover the same electric energy density as obtained within the fully quantum electromagnetic description. Finally, we show that for non-null coupling constants Schwinger particle production drastically reduces the electric energy density during the final stages of inflation.

</details>


### [134] [Extremal black hole throats from non extremal near horizon expansions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05460)
*Anirudhda Shinde,Mangesh Mandlik*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种从非极端近视界展开中恢复极端近视界几何的方法，通过表面重力、径向和时间的缩放，在高斯零坐标系下实现，并验证了该方法对多种黑洞解（如RN、Kerr、Kerr-Newman、带电旋转BTZ等）的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为统一处理具有平滑极端极限的稳态黑洞族的近视界几何结构，特别是如何从非极端情形过渡到极端情形。

Method: 在共用于黑洞族的高斯零坐标系下，结合表面重力的近极端缩放、径向缩放与Killing时间重缩放；保留二次径向项，忽略更高阶项；推导通用拉回公式提取近视界系数。

Result: 成功复现了Reissner-Nordström、Kerr、Kerr-Newman及带电旋转BTZ黑洞的已知近视界喉部结构；发现中性旋转BTZ情形下Carroll展开已足够。

Conclusion: 极端近视界几何可系统地由非极端近视界展开经适当缩放极限获得；二阶径向展开通常充分且必要（当二次项系数非零时）；该框架具有一般适用性与简洁性。

Abstract: We show how extremal near horizon geometries can be recovered from the non extremal near horizon expansion for smooth stationary black hole families admitting a smooth extremal limit. In Gaussian null coordinates common to the family, we apply the near extremal scaling of the surface gravity together with a radial scaling and a rescaling of Killing time. Although the physical surface gravity vanishes, the scaled horizon can retain a finite surface gravity. The extremal near horizon geometry is obtained when this scaled surface gravity is taken to zero. Under the near extremal scaling and the time rescaling, the quadratic radial term in the temporal metric component can survive, while higher radial orders vanish. Expansion through second order is therefore sufficient and is necessary whenever the quadratic term has a nonzero limiting coefficient in the chosen chart. We derive a common pullback prescription for extracting the near horizon coefficients and recover the known throats of Reissner-Nordström, Kerr, Kerr-Newman, and charged rotating Bañados-Teitelboim-Zanelli (BTZ) black holes. Neutral rotating BTZ provides a case in which the string Carroll expansion already suffices.

</details>


### [135] [More on the Celestial Soft Symmetry Algebra in the ${\cal N}=8$ Supergravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05760)
*Changhyun Ahn*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了N=8超引力中w_{1+∞}和ar{w}_{1+∞}电流与物质场的算符乘积展开（OPE），推导出相应的（反）对易关系，并通过三重（反）对易子验证了Jacobi恒等式，重现并发现了N=8天球软对称代数在全纯与反全纯扇区的两种形式；同时揭示了其包含N=8扩展BMS代数作为子代数，并通过软定理和shadow变换关联到应力-能量张量、超流及超平移生成元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示N=8超引力中天球上软对称性的完整代数结构，统一描述全纯与反全纯扇区的w_{1+∞}型软电流及其与BMS代数的关系，并连接软定理与shadow变换。

Method: 利用反全纯/全纯光锥变换构造N=8 w_{1+∞}和\bar{w}_{1+∞}电流，计算其与物质场的OPE；通过双围道积分得（反）对易子，三重（反）对易子检验Jacobi恒等式；结合shadow变换识别BMS生成元。

Result: 重现并新发现两类N=8天球软对称代数（分别位于反全纯与全纯扇区）；确认其均含N=8扩展BMS代数为子代数；导出引力子、引力微子和引力光子的非平凡软定理；通过shadow变换识别出应力-能量张量、超流和超平移生成元。

Conclusion: N=8超引力的天球对称性可由w_{1+∞}型软电流统一刻画，全纯与反全纯结构互补且对称，shadow变换自然导出BMS代数的核心生成元，表明w_{1+∞}代数是N=8天球散射振幅深层对称性的基本框架。

Abstract: We construct the operator product expansions (OPEs) of the ${\cal N}=8$ ${\rm w}_{1+\infty}$ currents (obtained from the antiholomorphic light transform) with the matter fields in the ${\cal N}=8$ supergravity. The corresponding (anti)commutators are obtained from the double contour integrals using the above OPEs. By computing the triple (anti)commutators consisting of current-current-matter (i.e., the Jacobi identity for fixed last element), the previously known ${\cal N}=8$ celestial soft symmetry algebra between these currents (in the antiholomorphic sector) is reproduced. We repeat the above procedure by taking the holomorphic light transform and the OPEs of the ${\cal N}=8$ ${\rm \bar{w}}_{1+\infty}$ currents with the matter fields and the corresponding (anti)commutators are obtained. We also obtain another type of ${\cal N}=8$ celestial soft symmetry algebra between these currents (in the holomorphic sector). As a subalgebra in the antiholomorphic and holomorphic sectors, there exists the ${\cal N}=8$ extended BMS (Bondi, van der Burg, Metzner, and Sachs in 1962) algebra. We analyze the above results from the ${\cal N}=8$ conformally {\it soft} ${\rm w}_{1+\infty}$ (or ${\rm \bar{w}}_{1+\infty}$) currents in the antiholomorphic (or holomorphic) sector. The nontrivial soft theorems for the graviton, the gravitinos and the graviphotons are described. Finally, using the shadow transform (the consecutive composition of holomorphic and antiholomorphic light transforms), we obtain the OPEs of the ${\cal N}=8$ {\it shadow} ${\rm w}_{1+\infty}$ (or ${\rm \bar{w}}_{1+\infty}$) currents with the matter fields where the stress-energy tensor, the supercurrents and the supertranslation generator of the ${\cal N}=8$ extended BMS algebra are identified.

</details>


### [136] [Einstein--Maxwell Sum Rules and the Persistence of the Smooth-Brane No-Go Theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05798)
*Paulo Michel Longo Tavares da Silva*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在五维弯曲膜世界中，最小耦合的阿贝尔麦克斯韦场能否缓解Gibbons-Kallosh-Linde（GKL）平滑性障碍；结果表明，在标准假设和四维庞加莱不变性下，背景中麦克斯韦场必须为零，因此GKL障碍依然存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究最小耦合阿贝尔麦克斯韦场是否能缓解五维 warped braneworld 中的 Gibbons--Kallosh--Linde (GKL) 平滑性障碍。

Method: 对与体麦克斯韦场耦合的标准标量场，推导保持切向F_{μν}与混合F_{μ5}分离的一参数全局相容性条件，并分析麦克斯韦应力张量在五维非迹零特性下的影响。

Result: 电磁项虽形式上修改积分GKL约束，但在标准假设及精确四维庞加莱不变性下，背景麦克斯韦场必须为零，故无法一致规避GKL不可行定理；平滑膜障碍依然成立。

Conclusion: 在标准框架下，麦克斯韦场不能缓解GKL障碍；要引入非平凡电磁贡献，必须放松如四维对称性或背景均匀性等关键假设。

Abstract: We investigate whether a minimally coupled Abelian Maxwell sector can relax the Gibbons--Kallosh--Linde (GKL) obstruction to smooth five-dimensional warped braneworlds. For a canonical scalar field coupled to a bulk Maxwell field, we derive the one-parameter family of global consistency conditions while keeping the tangential F_{μν} and mixed F_{μ5} sectors separate. This decomposition is essential because the Maxwell stress tensor is not traceless in five dimensions. We show that electromagnetic terms formally modify the integrated GKL constraints, but this modification does not provide a consistent evasion of the no-go theorem under the standard assumptions. Exact four-dimensional Poincaré invariance excludes a nonzero deterministic classical Maxwell field from the background, since its field strength would select preferred four-dimensional directions. The Maxwell sector therefore vanishes in the symmetric background and the usual smooth-brane obstruction is recovered. We also identify which assumptions must be relaxed for a nontrivial electromagnetic contribution and distinguish this background result from local consistency conditions for bulk gauge-field localization.

</details>


### [137] [Global Consistency of Cosmological Braneworlds in Palatini and Metric-Affine f(R) Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05821)
*Paulo Michel L. T da Silva*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了Palatini和度规仿射引力中五维翘曲膜世界的全局一致性条件，推导了宇宙翘曲几何的积分求和规则，并分析了独立联络自由度如何改变标准爱因斯坦引力对光滑紧致膜世界的阻碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决标准爱因斯坦引力中光滑紧致五维膜世界存在的‘不可行’障碍，探索非黎曼引力理论中可能的突破口。

Method: 基于广义Gibbons-Kallosh-Linde框架，分离几何、物质与非黎曼贡献，推导全局积分约束（sum rules），分析独立联络自由度的影响。

Result: 得到了适用于非黎曼引力的广义一致性关系，明确了偏离黎曼情形的关键项，并确立了检验五维光滑膜世界全局一致性的系统框架。

Conclusion: 非黎曼引力中的独立仿射结构可修改传统no-go结论，为构建自洽的五维翘曲膜世界提供了新的理论路径。

Abstract: We investigate global consistency conditions for five-dimensional warped braneworlds in Palatini and metric-affine gravity. Starting from the generalized Gibbons-Kallosh-Linde framework, we derive integrated sum rules for cosmological warped geometries and examine how independent connection degrees of freedom modify the standard Einstein-gravity obstruction to smooth compact braneworlds. The analysis separates geometric, matter, and non-Riemannian contributions and clarifies the conditions under which the usual no-go result can be modified. We show that the generalized consistency relations provide necessary global constraints on admissible configurations and identify the terms responsible for possible departures from the Riemannian case. The results establish a systematic framework for testing the global consistency of smooth five-dimensional braneworlds in gravitational theories with independent affine structure.

</details>


### [138] [Black Hole Entropy and Holographic Entanglement Entropy in DGP Brane Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05946)
*Takamasa Kanai*

Main category: hep-th
