<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究膜世界引力框架下引力熵与全息纠缠熵之间的内在联系，以深化对膜世界背景下全息原理的理解。

Method: 基于DGP膜模型及推广的高阶曲率引力（Lovelock和f(R)引力），通过分析膜上引力动力学与外曲率条件，推导引力熵与全息纠缠熵相等的判据。

Result: 发现引力熵与全息纠缠熵在静态膜世界黑洞下严格相等，且该等式条件在各类膜上引力理论中具有普适形式；但对一般稳态构型不自动成立。

Conclusion: 引力熵与全息纠缠熵的等价性在静态膜世界黑洞中成立，揭示了膜世界引力中全息性的一种具体实现方式，为理解引力与量子信息的深层联系提供了新视角。

Abstract: We investigate the relation between gravitational entropy and holographic entanglement entropy in braneworld gravity with gravitational dynamics localized on the brane. We first consider the DGP brane model, in which an Einstein-Hilbert term is included in the brane action, and derive the condition under which the gravitational entropy of a braneworld black hole agrees with the holographic entanglement entropy. We show that the two entropies coincide when a specific condition involving the extrinsic curvature of the brane is satisfied. In particular, this condition is automatically satisfied for static configurations.
  We then generalize the brane-localized Einstein-Hilbert term to higher-curvature gravitational theories. For Lovelock gravity and $f(R)$ gravity on the brane, we derive the corresponding conditions for the equality between gravitational entropy and holographic entanglement entropy and show that they take the same form as that obtained for Einstein gravity. Although the equality is not guaranteed for general stationary configurations, the required condition is satisfied for static braneworld black holes, leading to an agreement between the two entropies.
  These results clarify the relation between gravitational entropy and holographic entanglement entropy in braneworld gravity and contribute to a deeper understanding of holography in braneworld settings.

</details>


### [139] [IR flow of stochastic power spectra for interacting spectator scalars in the inflationary de Sitter spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05957)
*Vishal Nath,Sourav Bhattacharya*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了暴胀早期宇宙中相互作用的旁观者标量场理论下，宇宙关联函数在红外尺度流动下的行为。通过使用局部Schwinger-DeWitt展开传播子和精确de Sitter传播子，推导了φ⁴、φ⁴-Yukawa及破缺相O(N)模型的一圈有效势，并利用Wetterich方程研究其红外流导致的对称性恢复；进而计算了旁观者标量的随机功率谱与谱指数，发现不同传播子结果定性一致但定量差异显著，且谱指数在深红外区上升；还强调了费米子相互作用的影响，并指出大N极限下O(N)模型有效势对流变得不敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解暴胀时期旁观者标量场在红外尺度演化中的对称性行为及其对宇宙关联函数（如功率谱和谱指数）的影响，特别是量子修正与曲率效应在de Sitter时空中的作用。

Method: 采用局部Schwinger-DeWitt展开传播子和精确de Sitter传播子分别计算φ⁴、φ⁴-Yukawa和破缺相O(N)模型的一圈有效势；以之作为紫外边界条件代入Wetterich方程求解红外流；进而基于红外改进的有效势计算旁观者标量的随机双点关联函数、功率谱及谱指数。

Result: 有效势显示自发对称破缺，红外流驱动对称性恢复；功率谱的谱指数在深红外区对φ⁴和φ⁴-Yukawa模型均增大；费米子相互作用影响显著；O(N)模型中当N增大时，有效势形状对红外流趋于不敏感。

Conclusion: 红外流动显著改变早期宇宙标量场的对称性结构与关联性质，不同传播子方法虽定性一致但定量不可互换；费米子自由度与大N极限对红外行为具有关键调控作用，这对暴胀模型构建与CMB观测解释有重要启示。

Abstract: In this work we investigate the behaviour of early inflationary cosmic correlation functions under the flow of the infrared scale for interacting spectator scalar field theories. We start with the derivation of the one-loop effective potentials for $φ^4$, $φ^4$-Yukawa and broken phase $O(N)$ model in the inflationary de Sitter spacetime, first using the local Schwinger-DeWitt expansion propagator, and next using the exact de Sitter propagator. The Schwinger-DeWitt expansion being valid in a sub-Hubble length scale, furnishes suitable framework to determine the leading curvature corrections to high energy phenomena having sub-Hubble Compton wavelengths. These effective potentials show spontaneous symmetry breaking pattern, and are next used as ultraviolet boundary conditions in the Wetterich equation generating the desired IR flow. The flow is shown to drive the theory towards symmetry restoration. Using these IR-improved effective potentials, we next compute the stochastic power spectra and spectral indices for the temporally coincident two point correlation function of the spectator scalar. We show that the results corresponding to the aforementioned two kind of propagators are roughly in qualitative agreement with each other, but may differ quantitatively significantly. Both of them show increase in the spectral index in the deep IR for $φ^4$ and $φ^4$-Yukawa. The effect of fermionic interaction has been emphasised. Also for the case of the $O(N)$ scalar, we demonstrate that the effective potential's shape becomes more and more insensitive to the flow as $N$ gets large. Implication of this on the correlation function has been discussed.

</details>


### [140] [Momentum Flux for Small-Amplitude Oscillons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06224)
*N. S. Manton,Azadeh Mohammadi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了(1+1)维标量场理论中的振荡子（oscillon），计算了其周期平均动量流，并证明其随振幅ε呈高阶小量衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 振荡子是经典标量场中一种近似周期、局域振荡的解，缓慢辐射衰减；理解其动量辐射特性有助于揭示其准稳定性机制及与精确呼吸子（breather）的区别。

Method: 对小振幅ε的振荡子进行截断展开至ε^N阶，计算一个周期内的动量流积分，并分析其主导阶数依赖关系。

Result: 周期平均动量流：当N为偶数时主导阶为ε^{N+4}，N为奇数（除N=1外）时为ε^{N+5}，而N=1时为ε^4；整体极其微小。

Conclusion: 振荡子的动量辐射被高度压制，表明其具有极强的准守恒动量特性，接近理想呼吸子行为。

Abstract: An oscillon in scalar field theory in $(1+1)$-dimensions is an oscillating, localised, almost periodic classical solution that slowly radiates and decays to the vacuum solution. We calculate its momentum flux integrated over a period, which would be zero for an exact breather. For an oscillon of small amplitude $\eps$, truncated at order $\eps^N$, we show that the integrated momentum flux is of leading order $\eps^{N+4}$ for even $N$ and $\eps^{N+5}$ for odd $N$, except for $N=1$, where it is of order $\eps^4$. It is therefore very small.

</details>


### [141] [Nonlocal Dirac theory in curved spacetime and the Bunch--Davies vacuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06390)
*Paulo José Porfírio,Ramires Nascimento da Silva*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在弯曲时空中的非局域自旋-1/2场论，使用基于协变Dirac算子的非多项式整函数作为形因子；推导了相应的非局域Dirac方程，并研究其与引力和U(1)规范场的耦合；在de Sitter时空开图中构造了非局域Bunch-Davies模式，发现其动力学可映射为具有效质量Mₙ的局域Dirac方程，且Mₙ由Lambert-W函数分支给出；保持了Euclidean解析结构与物理正残差分支上的量子效应（如Bogoliubov系数、纠缠熵等）的非局域修正，并在Λ→∞极限下平滑回归局域理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决局域量子场论在普朗克尺度可能失效的问题，通过引入非局域性（以整个Dirac算子的非多项式函数为形因子）来构建更基本的弯曲时空费米子理论，并保持良好紫外行为与物理一致性。

Method: 构建基于协变Dirac算子D的非局域形因子F(D)，导出非局域Dirac方程；在de Sitter时空开图中求解该方程，构造非局域Bunch-Davies真空模；利用Lambert-W函数分析有效质量谱；检验Euclidean解析性、Bogoliubov变换及量子信息量（如纠缠熵）的非局域修正。

Result: 非局域Dirac动力学可逐一分支映射为具离散有效质量Mₙ的局域方程；Mₙ由Lambert-W函数分支精确给出；Bunch-Davies构造的Euclidean解析性得以保持；物理分支上所有关键量子效应（挤压参数、占据数、纠缠熵）均获得显式非局域修正；无超曲率费米模式仍成立；局域极限Λ→∞被平滑恢复。

Conclusion: 该非局域自旋-1/2场论为弯曲时空中的紫外完备费米子理论提供了自洽框架，兼具数学良定义性、物理可观测性与向局域理论的自然过渡，支持其作为量子引力低能有效描述的候选。

Abstract: We formulate a nonlocal spin-$1/2$ field theory in curved spacetime in which the form factor is a nonpolynomial entire function of the covariant Dirac operator. We derive the corresponding nonlocal Dirac equation and analyze its coupling to gravity and to a $\rm U(1)$ gauge field. As an application, we study the theory in the open chart of de Sitter spacetime and construct the associated nonlocal Bunch--Davies modes. The nonlocal dynamics can be mapped, branch by branch, onto the local Dirac equation with an effective mass $M_n$. For the exponential form factors considered here, the effective masses are expressed in terms of branches of the Lambert-$W$ function. We show that the Euclidean analyticity structure of the Bunch--Davies construction is preserved. On the physical positive-residue branch, the Bogoliubov coefficients, squeezing parameter, occupation number, and entanglement entropy acquire explicit nonlocal corrections, while the absence of physical fermionic supercurvature modes is preserved. The local theory is smoothly recovered in the limit $Λ\to\infty$.

</details>


### [142] [A local second law for black hole dynamics in the charged large-$D$ membrane paradigm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06470)
*Supratim Halder,Mangesh Mandlik*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在大维度膜范式下，通过计算带电黑洞膜速度散度的次主导阶修正，并结合爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦约束与非仿射零测地线Raychaudhuri方程，导出了膜熵流并证明其散度非负，从而确立了带电大D膜范式的局域第二定律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管中性膜的局域热力学第二定律已被建立，但带电大D膜框架下的局域熵产生方程仍悬而未决。

Method: 计算带电黑洞在平直背景中膜速度散度的次主导阶修正；利用非仿射零测地线Raychaudhuri方程，在物理事件视界上评估爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦标量约束；将物理视界量映射到辅助膜运动学；构造膜熵流。

Result: 发现膜速度散度在膜世界体积首阶非零项中非负，由膜剪切和麦克斯韦场新贡献两个二次型驱动；由此构造的膜熵流散度亦非负。

Conclusion: 成功确立了带电大D膜范式的局域热力学第二定律。

Abstract: The large-$D$ membrane paradigm provides a perturbative, non-gravitational description of black hole dynamics. While the local second law of thermodynamics has been established for neutral membranes, the explicit determination of the local entropy production equation within the charged large-$D$ membrane framework remains unsolved. In this paper, we compute the subleading-order correction to the divergence of the membrane velocity for a charged black hole in a flat background. We evaluate the corresponding scalar Einstein-Maxwell constraint equation at the physical event horizon via the non-affine null Raychaudhuri equation, and relate the physical horizon expressions to the auxiliary membrane kinematics, demonstrating that the membrane velocity produces a non-negative divergence at the first non-vanishing order in the membrane worldvolume, which is driven by two quadratic forms: the membrane shear and a novel contribution generated purely by the Maxwell field. Using this result, we construct the membrane entropy current and prove that its divergence is non-negative through this order, thereby establishing the local second law for the charged large-$D$ membrane paradigm.

</details>


### [143] [Quantum Gravity in the Landau background gauge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06564)
*J. Frenkel,S. Martins-Filho*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在朗道背景规范下研究了引力场和物质场的量子理论的有效作用量，分析了BRST不变性及相关的Ward恒等式，并在一阶圈图水平上显式计算了壳内和壳外抵消项拉格朗日量；同时指出物质自能在此规范下在一阶有限，可能在高阶圈图中也保持有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨引力与物质场量子理论在特定规范（朗道背景规范）下的可重整性与对称性保护机制，特别是关注有效作用量的BRST不变性和Ward恒等式的实现。

Method: 采用背景场方法结合BRST对称性分析，在朗道背景规范下推导并计算一阶圈图贡献，显式构造壳内与壳外抵消项拉格朗日量。

Result: 在一阶圈图水平上得到了完整的抵消项拉格朗日量；发现物质自能在该规范下有限，并推测其可能在所有阶都有限。

Conclusion: 朗道背景规范为研究量子引力与物质耦合系统提供了良好的对称性控制框架，物质自能的有限性暗示该规范可能有助于改善紫外行为。

Abstract: We examine, in the Landau background gauge, the effective action of a quantum theory of gravitational and matter fields. We analyse the BRST invariance of this theory and study the Ward identities which implement the symmetry of the background effective action. The off-shell and on-shell counterterm Lagrangians are explicitly calculated in this gauge at one-loop order. We argue that the matter self-energy in the Landau background gauge, which is finite to this order, may be finite also at higher loops.

</details>


### [144] [de Sitter Wavefunction from Quadrangular Polylogarithms: Polygon Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06772)
*Livia Ferro,Tomasz Lukowski,Shruti Paranjape,Lecheng Ren,Marcus Spradlin,Anastasia Volovich,He-Chen Weng,Yao-Qi Zhang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文推导了de Sitter空间中共形耦合φ³理论宇宙波函数n边形图贡献的显式公式，利用B_{2n−1}簇代数全兼容性，并引入B型四边形多对数函数来表达该系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为精确计算de Sitter空间中φ³理论宇宙波函数的高阶图贡献，并揭示其与簇代数及特殊多对数函数之间的深层数学结构联系。

Method: 基于符号全兼容性（total compatibility）性质，构造B型四边形多对数函数（源于4n边形中心对称四边剖分），并通过递归微分方程与多对数函数的余乘积递归公式建立对应，辅以软极限确定积分常数。

Result: 给出了n-边形图对宇宙波函数被积函数的显式解析表达式，证明其可表示为权重为n的B型四边形多对数函数。

Conclusion: n边形波函数系数具有由B_{2n−1}簇代数控制的精确组合结构，B型四边形多对数函数为其自然数学实现，统一了递归结构与代数几何性质。

Abstract: We present an explicit formula for the $n$-gon graph contribution to the integrand of the cosmological wavefunction for conformally coupled $φ^3$ theory in de Sitter space. Our result relies on the fact that its symbol satisfies total compatibility with respect to the $B_{2n-1}$ cluster algebra. We introduce $B$-type quadrangular polylogarithms, constructed from centrally symmetric quadrangulations of a $4n$-gon, and show that the $n$-gon wavefunction coefficient is expressed by a natural weight-$n$ function of this class. We prove our formula by directly relating the recursive differential equation satisfied by these wavefunction coefficients to a recursive coproduct formula for $B$-type quadrangular polylogarithms, with the remaining additive constant fixed by the soft limit.

</details>


<div id='astro-ph.CO'></div>

# astro-ph.CO [[Back]](#toc)

### [145] [Map-based asymmetric beam treatment: application of real-space beam patterns in CMB data analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03840)
*Artem Basyrov*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于时间序列数据（TOD）的map-domain方法，用于精确处理真实望远镜非对称光束引起的模式耦合与I→P泄漏问题，并通过无噪声验证表明其在ℓ ≲ 1000范围内准确复现温度与B模功率谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实望远镜光束具有不对称性，破坏了球谐空间中简单的Bℓ因子化，导致ℓ和m模式间耦合及强度到偏振（I→P）泄漏，影响CMB B模探测精度。

Method: 在map-domain中将光束卷积建模为依赖扫描轨迹的局部操作，直接作用于时间序列数据（TOD），完整包含光束形状信息；并将其形式化为稀疏线性BeamOperator，以支持模块化制图。

Result: 无噪声验证显示该方法能精确复现输入温度与B模功率谱至ℓ ≃ 1000，显著超出原初B模科学所需ℓ范围；且可自然处理I→P泄漏。

Conclusion: 所提TOD域光束处理方法比传统球谐空间近似更精确、灵活，适用于高精度CMB偏振分析，尤其对抑制系统误差至关重要。

Abstract: Real telescope beams are asymmetric, breaking the simple $B_{\ell}$ factorisation in harmonic space and coupling modes across $\ell$ and $m$. We develop a map-domain treatment in which beam convolution is a local, scan-dependent operation applied directly to the time-ordered data (TOD), accommodating the full beam shape and giving a direct handle on intensity-to-polarisation ($I \rightarrow P$) leakage. A noiseless validation against spherical-harmonic convolution reproduces the input temperature and $B$-mode power spectra up to $\ell \simeq 1000$, well beyond the range of primordial $B$-mode science. We outline how the operation maps onto a sparse linear BeamOperator for modular map-making.

</details>


### [146] [Anisotropic cosmic birefringence estimates for forthcoming CMB experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03876)
*Matteo Longo Minnolo,Matteo Billi,Giuseppe Puglisi*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了宇宙双折射（CB）的各向异性成分，提出了一种基于天区分块的局域化方法来重建各向异性旋转角，并预测了未来CMB实验（如Simons Observatory和LiteBIRD）对CB各向异性的探测灵敏度，显著优于当前Planck结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 各向异性CB是区分均匀类精质场与空间变化的轴子类暗物质的关键；但目前尚未被探测到。

Method: 采用基于天区分块的局域化技术，在模拟CMB加噪声图上用EB D-估计器构建χ²_EB统计量，最小化该量以重建局域旋转角。

Result: 预测卫星任务（如LiteBIRD）可将CB各向异性统计误差比Planck提升26–40倍，地面任务（如Simons Observatory SAT）提升11–15倍；对应尺度不变振幅95%置信上限分别为3.5×10⁻⁴ deg²和7.1×10⁻⁴ deg²，较Planck改进约20倍和10倍。

Conclusion: 未来CMB实验有望显著提升对各向异性CB的限制能力，为探索超越标准模型的新物理提供关键约束。

Abstract: Cosmic Birefringence (CB) is a unique probe of physics beyond the Standard Model, potentially sourced by an axion-like field coupled to photons via a Chern-Simons interaction. Recent Planck analyses have strengthened evidence for a non-zero isotropic rotation angle, yet its anisotropic component remains undetected, despite being the key to distinguishing a homogeneous quintessence-like field from spatially varying axion-like dark matter. We forecast the sensitivity of forthcoming ground- and space-based CMB experiments to anisotropic CB, applying a patch-based localization technique to simulated CMB-plus-noise maps with white noise levels representative of Simons Observatory SAT- and LiteBIRD-like sensitivities. Local rotation angles are reconstructed on independent sky patches by minimizing the $χ^2_{EB}$ statistic built from the $EB$ D-estimator. Under our idealized simulations, the forecast angular power spectrum improves current Planck-based constraints on the CB anisotropy statistical uncertainty by a factor of 26-40 for a satellite-like mission and 11-15 for a realistic ground-based footprint. Translating these into constraints on the scale-invariant amplitude, fitting the reconstructed bandpowers with a scale-invariant amplitude model, we forecast with a 95 \% C.L. $A_{\rm CB} < 3.5\times10^{-4}\,{\rm deg}^2$ for the space-like mission and $A_{\rm CB} < 7.1\times10^{-4}\,{\rm deg}^2$ for the ground-based footprint, improving on the current full-sky Planck constraint by a factor of about 20 and 10, respectively. These values represent upper bounds on the achievable gain, and indicate that forthcoming CMB experiments will be able to further shed light on anisotropic CB.

</details>


### [147] [Weak lensing, Big Magnification: Recovering hidden lensing information from galaxy positions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03913)
*Alex Alarcon,Carles Sánchez*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为BigMag的新方法，通过按放大响应符号和幅度对高红移背景星系进行分类，以减少正负放大响应的信号抵消，从而提升弱引力透镜放大效应的信噪比和宇宙学约束能力；该方法结合逆星系-星系透镜（IGGL），可实现与传统3×2pt分析相当甚至更优的S8约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统弱引力透镜（基于剪切）信噪比高，但放大效应（magnification）因正负响应星系相互抵消而信噪比较低；本文旨在提升放大效应作为独立宇宙学探针的有效性。

Method: 利用DES Year 6测光数据与Balrog模拟源注入，对z≈0.7–2.5背景星系按其放大响应的符号与幅度进行划分，构建响应选择的前景-背景交叉相关（BigMag）；同时结合逆星系-星系透镜（IGGL），并用于自校准放大响应；还构造近零响应样本以支持传统3×2pt与几何透镜比分析。

Result: BigMag在理想样本构型与精确响应标定下，宇宙学约束（尤其是S8）可达甚至优于传统2×2pt；BigMag+IGGL联合分析可实现与传统3×2pt相当或更紧的S8约束；同时支持零响应样本用于其他透镜分析。

Conclusion: 按放大响应分类星系能显著提升透镜放大效应的实用性；BigMag及其与IGGL的组合为未来巡天提供了一种有潜力的、仅依赖位置信息的互补宇宙学探针，需进一步优化星系选择与验证。

Abstract: Galaxy clustering and weak gravitational lensing are powerful cosmological probes, traditionally combined through galaxy clustering and galaxy-galaxy lensing in a $2\times2$pt analysis. An alternative is the foreground-background clustering cross-correlation induced by lensing magnification, but measurements have achieved lower signal-to-noise than shear-based lensing. We show that, for deep photometric samples, cancellation between galaxies with positive and negative magnification responses can suppress this signal. Using DES Year 6 photometry and Balrog synthetic-source injections, we explore high-redshift ($z\simeq0.7$-$2.5$) samples split by the sign and amplitude of their magnification response. This separation reduces cancellation and can increase both signal-to-noise and cosmological constraining power. We call the combination of foreground clustering and foreground-background cross-correlations with response-selected background samples BigMag. Forecasts with DES-like simulated data vectors show that, for an optimistic sample configuration and sufficiently precise response calibration, BigMag can achieve constraining power comparable to traditional $2\times2$pt using galaxy positions alone. The same samples also enhance inverse galaxy-galaxy lensing (IGGL), a position-shear correlation dominated by magnification when the position sample lies behind the shape sample. Combining BigMag and IGGL further self-calibrates the magnification responses and yields marginalized $S_8$ constraints comparable to traditional $3\times2$pt analyses, and tighter for the more precise calibration scenarios considered here. The framework also enables near-zero-response samples for traditional $3\times2$pt and geometric lensing-ratio analyses. These results motivate further optimization and validation of galaxy selections to realize magnification as a complementary cosmological probe. [Abrg.]

</details>


### [148] [Baryonic Imprints on DM Halos: characterizing the full concentration-mass probability distribution with CAMELS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03946)
*Elisa J. Gao,Dhayaa Anbajagane*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文利用CAMELS模拟套件中的IllustrisTNG运行，结合归一化流模型，系统研究了晕浓度-质量关系的前四阶矩（均值、弥散、偏度、峰度）对宇宙学与天体物理参数的依赖性，发现高阶矩在银河系质量尺度上对重子分数和超新星反馈高度敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管晕浓度-质量关系的平均行为与天体物理过程的联系已取得进展，但其高阶统计矩（如弥散、偏度、峰度）对天体物理与宇宙学参数的依赖仍缺乏系统研究。

Method: 使用CAMELS中的IllustrisTNG和ASTRID模拟数据，采用归一化流（normalizing flows）建模浓度分布，作为晕质量、红移及六个宇宙学/天体物理参数的函数；分析质量范围10^11–10^14.5 M⊙/h、红移z=0–1内的前四阶矩。

Result: 所有高阶矩均呈现质量依赖的宇宙学与天体物理敏感性；弥散在银河系质量尺度出现非单调行为，受重子分数和超新星反馈显著调控；偏度主要由重子分数主导；峰度行为与偏度一致；ASTRID模拟验证了结果稳健性。

Conclusion: 晕浓度分布的高阶矩（尤其弥散与偏度）是探测星系形成物理（特别是重子反馈）和宇宙学参数的重要新探针，尤其适用于银河系及更低质量晕的统计分析。

Abstract: The halo concentration, which represents the distribution of matter within a halo, is a vital tool for modeling the matter distribution on scales ranging from megaparsecs to sub-kiloparsecs. While significant progress has been made on connecting the impact of astrophysics on the mean concentration as a function of halo mass, the same connection to higher moments of the concentration distribution remains far less explored. We employ the IllustrisTNG runs in the CAMELS simulation suite to simultaneously quantify the astrophysics and cosmology dependence of the first four moments of the concentration-mass relation; the mean, the scatter, the skewness, and the kurtosis. We use normalizing flows to model the concentration distribution as a function of halo mass, redshift, and six cosmological/astrophysical parameters. We focus on the mass range $M_{\rm vir} \in [10^{11}, 10^{14.5}] M_{\odot}/h$, and the redshift range $z \in [0, 1]$. All higher-order moments exhibit a mass-dependent sensitivity to both astrophysics and cosmology. The behavior in dark matter-only simulations follows the known effects of active mergers and of tidal stripping in smaller halos. Once galaxy formation is included, the scatter shows non-monotonic behavior at the Milky Way (MW) mass-scale, and the amplitude of this behavior is strongly influenced by the baryon fraction and supernovae (SNe) feedback parameters. The skewness has a large sensitivity to the baryon fraction as well and is mostly insensitive to the other parameters, while the kurtosis follows the behavior set by the skewness. Results for the ASTRID simulation suite show similar behavior as that of IllustrisTNG. The cosmology and astrophysics dependence of these higher-order moments impact analyses that are sensitive to the full distribution of the concentration-mass relation, particularly for halos at/below the MW mass scale.

</details>


### [149] [Stars Would Remember a Recent Gravitational Transition: Testing G-step Resolutions of the Hubble Tension with Red Dwarfs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04048)
*Jeremy Sakstein,Harry Desmond*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文通过分析红矮星的半径变化，检验了牛顿引力常数G在73-130百万年前可能发生快速跃变的假设，结果以显著性3.1σ和4.0σ排除了为解决哈勃张力所提出的两种G跃变模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为检验某些解决哈勃张力方案中提出的牛顿引力常数G在约73–130百万年前发生快速跃变的假设。

Method: 利用质量低于约0.35倍太阳质量的全对流红矮星（其热时标与所提跃变回溯时间相当）对G变化的记忆效应，计算G阶跃变化对其当前半径的影响，并与8颗处于分离食双星系统中的红矮星的质量-半径观测数据进行比对。

Result: 所提G跃变模型（73 Myr前G增加4%，130 Myr前增加11%）分别被排除，置信度达3.1σ和4.0σ。

Conclusion: 观测数据不支持为缓解哈勃张力而提出的近期G快速跃变假说，从而对相关新物理模型施加了强约束。

Abstract: We test the hypothesis that Newton's gravitational constant underwent a rapid transition approximately $73$-$130\,{\rm Myr}$ ago, as proposed in some resolutions of the Hubble tension. Fully convective red dwarf stars with masses below $\sim0.35\,M_\odot$ have thermal timescales comparable to the proposed transition look-back times, so an appreciable present-day memory can remain. We show that a rapid step in $G$ induces measurable changes in their radii and confront these predictions with mass-radius measurements of eight red dwarfs in detached eclipsing binaries. We find that the $G$-step models proposed to resolve the Hubble tension, corresponding to a $4\%$ change in $G$ at a look-back time of $73\,{\rm Myr}$ and an $11\%$ change at $130\,{\rm Myr}$, are excluded at significances of $3.1σ$ and $4.0σ$, respectively.

</details>


### [150] [Beyond dust in local cosmography: covariant kinetic theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04284)
*Chris Clarkson,Roy Maartens*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种协变动力学框架，用于描述冷暗物质在流线交叉和速度弥散下的非测地运动，通过Vlasov方程的动量矩展开与截断，导出了能量密度、各向同性压强与各向异性压强的动力学闭合系统，并精确求解了动量约束，进而将非测地流引入局域宇宙学观测（如哈勃偶极、减速多极），为用距离-红移数据直接检验无碰撞暗物质提供了理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冷暗物质作为‘尘埃’建模虽简化问题但忽略流线交叉与速度弥散效应，导致无法刻画真实碰撞less系统的非测地运动及由此产生的局域哈勃流偶极等可观测效应；需发展不依赖背景时空或对称性假设的协变动理学框架。

Method: 从精确的有质量Vlasov层级方程出发，构建能量积分后的速度矩序列，并按速度权重组织；在四极矩处自洽截断以闭合动力学系统；精确求解动量约束得到物质参考系中的四维加速度；推导含速度弥散贡献的涡度演化非线性表达式；并将该非测地流纳入协变局域宇宙学分析框架。

Result: 得到了物质能量密度、各向同性压强与各向异性压强的闭合动力学方程组；获得了动量约束的精确非线性解（即四维加速度）；揭示了速度弥散通过四维加速度旋度激发涡度的机制；明确了四维加速度作为局域哈勃偶极源及减速多极贡献者的角色；建立了尘埃宇宙学的协变动理学延拓。

Conclusion: 该框架为冷暗物质在非线性结构形成阶段的协变描述提供了严格基础，使局域距离-红移观测可直接约束碰撞less相空间动力学，弥补了传统尘埃模型在宇宙学观测解释上的根本缺陷。

Abstract: Modelling cold dark matter as dust makes the matter congruence geodesic and therefore removes any intrinsic dipole from the local Hubble flow. The dust model breaks down as stream crossing develops, whereas a collisionless gas accommodates stream crossing via velocity dispersion in phase space. We develop a covariant kinetic framework for this regime without assuming a background spacetime or any geometrical symmetry. Starting from the exact massive Vlasov hierarchy, we construct its energy-integrated moments and organise these by velocity weight. A self-consistent truncation at the quadrupole closes the dynamics on the matter energy density, isotropic pressure and anisotropic pressure. Their gradients generate a matter-frame 4-acceleration, for which we find an exact nonlinear solution of the momentum constraint, valid in the general case without truncation. This goes beyond previous work in which an arbitrary 4-acceleration is introduced. Velocity dispersion also sources vorticity through the curl of 4-acceleration, and we derive the nonlinear expression for vorticity evolution. We then propagate the non-geodesic matter flow into covariant local cosmography, where the 4-acceleration is the intrinsic Hubble dipole and also enters the deceleration multipoles. Our construction provides a covariant kinetic completion of dust cosmography and identifies the quantities required to test collisionless matter directly with local distance--redshift data. A companion paper specialises the hierarchy to perturbed FLRW spacetime and predicts the size of the resulting cosmographic signals.

</details>


### [151] [Forecasting cosmological constraints from the nonlinear halo density field with Zel'dovich-inspired transform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04486)
*Yu Zhou,Yun Wang,Haozhe Zhang,Matteo Peron,Shiyu Deng,Yu Yu,Xin Zhang*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Zel'dovich启发的非线性密度场变换（ZI变换），通过调节参数η来提取高阶关联信息，并在红移空间中利用其单极功率谱显著提升对宇宙学参数（如中微子质量、原初非高斯性等）的约束精度，尤其在引入尺度依赖噪声校正后效果更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为克服标准两体统计（如常规晕功率谱）在提取非线性宇宙学信息方面的局限性，需发展能有效恢复高阶N点关联信息的新统计量。

Method: 采用Zel'dovich启发（ZI）变换对密度场进行局域非线性变换，结合Quijote及Quijote-PNG模拟中的晕样本，分析其红移空间单极功率谱；使用Fisher矩阵方法评估不同η取值（1、10、+∞）组合的联合统计量P_ZI,0，并引入尺度依赖的噪声校正。

Result: ZI变换功率谱P_ZI,0相较常规晕功率谱P_h,0，在约束f_NL^equil、f_NL^ortho、M_ν和n_s上分别提升5.6、5.5、5.2和5.8倍；且整体优于标记功率谱；噪声校正进一步收紧约束。

Conclusion: ZI功率谱是一种极具潜力的补充性统计工具，可有效增强对非线性宇宙学信息的提取能力，尤其适用于未来高密度星系巡天。

Abstract: The Zel'dovich-inspired (ZI) transform is a local nonlinear transform of the density field that can Gaussianize the late-time matter field and recover information from higher-order $N$-point correlation functions through its power spectrum, with the parameter $η$ controlling the weighting of higher-order information. Using Fisher-matrix analyses of halo catalogs from the Quijote and Quijote-PNG simulations, we study the redshift-space monopole power spectrum of the ZI-transformed halo density field. The joint statistic $P_{\rm ZI,0}$, constructed from three representative transforms with $η=1$, $10$, and $+\infty$, yields substantially tighter constraints than the conventional halo power spectrum $P_{\rm h,0}$ on standard cosmological parameters, the summed neutrino mass, and primordial non-Gaussianity amplitudes, while outperforming the marked power spectrum for many parameters. We further calibrate a scale-dependent noise correction for the ZI power spectrum, with an effective noise amplitude that depends on wavenumber, and find that the correction further tightens the constraints. In particular, the noise-corrected $P_{\rm ZI,0}$ improves the constraints on $f_{\rm NL}^{\rm equil}$, $f_{\rm NL}^{\rm ortho}$, $M_ν$, and $n_{\rm s}$ by factors of $5.6$, $5.5$, $5.2$, and $5.8$, respectively, relative to $P_{\rm h,0}$. These results demonstrate the potential of ZI power spectra as a complement to standard two-point statistics for extracting nonlinear cosmological information. Our forecasts may be conservative because the halo catalogs have substantially lower number densities than those expected from forthcoming galaxy surveys.

</details>


### [152] [Measuring the Moving Lens Effect with Wide-Separation Lensed Quasars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04865)
*Yupeng Zhang,Shuxun Tian,Zhengxiang Li*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于宽分离引力透镜类星体的光谱学方法，用于探测移动透镜效应（moving-lens effect），即前景透镜横向运动引起的光子频率偏移；以SDSS J1004+4112为例，通过C IV和C III]发射线联合分析，在ELT级望远镜40小时积分下，光子噪声极限精度可达σ_z≈2.5×10⁻⁷，足以分辨典型信号|Δz_ML|≈6.7×10⁻⁷，表明该方法在统计上可行，但实际探测需压制天体物理与仪器系统误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动透镜效应可探测量子尺度上的前景物质横向本动速度，已有CMB-LSS交叉相关给出统计证据，但缺乏直接、高精度的光谱学验证手段；宽分离透镜类星体不同像对应不同偏折几何，为差分红移测量提供天然实验室。

Method: 聚焦于强透镜类星体SDSS J1004+4112，构建融合宽发射线轮廓信息的差分红shift估计量；采用仅含光子噪声的蒙特卡洛模拟，在ELT类观测配置下分别预报C IV与C III]谱线在40小时积分下的测量精度。

Result: 对500 km/s横向速度及最优朝向，预期移动透镜差分红移|Δz_ML|≈6.7×10⁻⁷；40小时积分下联合精度σ_z≈2.5×10⁻⁷；C IV主导约束，C III]贡献有限；数十小时积分即可进入10⁻⁷精度量级。

Conclusion: 光子统计极限已足以分辨典型移动透镜信号，证实该光谱学路径原理可行；但实际探测成败取决于对天体物理（如宽线区动力学）与仪器系统误差（如波长定标）的精确建模与控制。

Abstract: The transverse motion of a gravitational potential induces frequency shifts in photons propagating through it, giving rise to the moving-lens effect and providing a probe of transverse peculiar velocities. Statistical evidence for this effect has recently been reported using cross-correlations between cosmic microwave background temperature maps and large-scale-structure tracers. Here, we investigate a complementary spectroscopic approach based on wide-separation lensed quasars, for which different lensed images probe distinct deflection geometries through a moving foreground lens. We focus on SDSS J1004+4112 and assess the differential redshift that could be measured from its broad emission-line spectra. Using an estimator that combines information across the line profiles, we perform Monte Carlo forecasts for C IV and C III] under an ELT-like configuration, considering photon noise only. For an assumed lens transverse velocity of $500\,{\rm km\,s^{-1}}$ and the orientation maximizing the signal, the expected differential redshift reaches $|Δz_{\rm ML}|\simeq6.7\times10^{-7}$. At an integration time of 40 hr, the joint photon-noise-limited precision is $σ_z\simeq2.5\times10^{-7}$. C IV dominates the constraint, while C III] provides only a modest additional improvement, and integrations of tens of hours reach the $10^{-7}$ precision regime. These results show that photon statistics alone can reach the characteristic moving-lens signal scale, although practical detectability will depend on controlling astrophysical and instrumental systematics.

</details>


### [153] [Limits on the size of electron density fluctuations in the early universe and implications for the Hubble tension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05147)
*Gabriel P. Lynch,Jens Chluba,Richard Battye,Geoffrey Vasil*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了早期宇宙中小于CMB分辨率尺度的电子密度涨落对CMB的影响，提出了改进的Boltzmann代码classito，并利用Planck、ACT DR6和SPT-3G数据首次给出了相关限制；结果未发现显著证据，且重新评估了此类涨落是否能解决哈勃张力问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索小尺度（千秒差距至兆秒差距）电子密度涨落在ΛCDM扩展模型中的存在性及其对CMB观测的影响，特别是其对哈勃常数H₀推断及哈勃张力问题的潜在作用。

Method: 在修改版Boltzmann代码classito中同时纳入电子密度涨落导致的加速复合效应与沿视线方向相关性引起的汤姆孙散射率降低效应，并结合Planck、ACT DR6和SPT-3G D1的CMB数据进行参数限制与拟合分析。

Result: 未发现电子密度涨落的显著证据：电子密度对比度方差95%置信上限为0.21，当相干长度为2 Mpc时降至0.033；在k≈1–100 Mpc⁻¹范围内给出电子功率谱限制；ACT与SPT-3G数据打破H₀与涨落参数的简并，使H₀估计值回落至与ΛCDM一致，不支持涨落缓解哈勃张力。

Conclusion: 小尺度电子密度涨落目前无观测支持，且不能稳健地解释哈勃张力；本工作提供了可公开使用的工具和框架，使相关理论模型能被CMB数据直接检验。

Abstract: Little is known about the early universe on scales smaller than those resolved by the cosmic microwave background (CMB), where electron density fluctuations on kiloparsec to megaparsec scales are expected in many extensions of LCDM. These fluctuations nonetheless leave two imprints on the CMB. They accelerate recombination, the effect behind previous constraints and proposals to raise the inferred Hubble constant, $H_0$. Through correlations along the line of sight, they also reduce the effective Thomson scattering rate, enhancing diffusion damping by an amount that grows with their coherence length. We implement both effects in a modified Boltzmann code, classito, and use CMB data from Planck, ACT DR6, and SPT-3G D1 to place the first limits on such fluctuations that include this modified photon transport. We find no evidence for them: the variance of the electron density contrast is below $0.21$ ($95\%$ CL), falling to $0.033$ for a coherence length of $2\,{\rm Mpc}$, and we present these limits in terms of the electron power spectrum at $1\,{\rm Mpc}^{-1}\lesssim k\lesssim10^2\,{\rm Mpc}^{-1}$. We also reassess small-scale electron density fluctuations as a resolution of the Hubble tension. In Planck data the transport effect is partially degenerate with accelerated recombination, widening the range of $H_0$ allowed: combined with a distance-ladder prior, Planck gives $H_0 = 71.1\pm0.7\,{\rm km\,s^{-1}\,Mpc^{-1}}$ and an improved fit relative to LCDM. ACT and SPT-3G break this degeneracy, chiefly through the temperature--$E$-mode correlation in the damping tail, and with the same prior they give only $H_0 = 69.3\pm0.5\,{\rm km\,s^{-1}\,Mpc^{-1}}$ and no preference over LCDM. With our treatment and publicly available code, models that generate small-scale electron density fluctuations can be directly confronted with CMB data through the variance and coherence length that they predict.

</details>


### [154] [Kinetic-theory derivation and Bayesian analysis of the finite-temperature shifted CMB power spectrum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05435)
*I. Y. Park*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限温度下量子引力对宇宙学常数的修正所引入的两个额外密度参数Ω_{Λ_2}和Ω_{Λ_3}，通过贝叶斯MCMC分析和自建CMB动力学理论管道，发现二者均与零一致，且其信号被哈勃参数h和功率谱整体归一化高度简并，导致观测上无法显著探测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究有限温度量子引力修正对宇宙学常数的影响，特别是新引入的a^{-4}和a^{-2}标度密度参数Ω_{Λ_2}和Ω_{Λ_3}是否可被当前CMB数据（Planck 2018）约束。

Method: 1) 使用修改版CLASS代码进行贝叶斯MCMC分析；2) 基于Weinberg动理学理论从头构建独立CMB计算管道，并开展响应分析以理解物理机制和参数简并性。

Result: Ω_{Λ_2}和Ω_{Λ_3}均与零一致；Ω_{Λ_3}仅单边受限；其CMB信号主要被h偏移吸收，残余部分位于低ℓ区（受宇宙方差主导）；Ω_{Λ_2}与功率谱总振幅高度简并；二者效应微弱且难以区分。

Conclusion: 由于高阶热修正效应微弱，且与标准宇宙学参数（尤其是h和A_s）存在强简并，当前CMB数据尚无法可靠探测Ω_{Λ_2}和Ω_{Λ_3}，未发现显著非零证据。

Abstract: Finite-temperature quantum-gravitational corrections to the cosmological constant introduce two additional density parameters, $Ω_{Λ_2}$ and $Ω_{Λ_3}$, the associated terms of which scale as $a^{-4}$ and $a^{-2}$, respectively. In previous work (Papers I and II), these parameters were studied using machine-learning methods that identified a best-fit point. Here we complete the picture with two complementary analyses. First, we perform a Bayesian MCMC analysis using a modified version of \textsc{class} against the Planck 2018 likelihoods. This tells us what the data can constrain once all parameter uncertainties have been marginalized over. Both parameters are consistent with zero, with $Ω_{Λ_3}$ constrained only from one side. Second, we build a CMB pipeline from scratch, following Weinberg's kinetic-theory formulation and validating it against \textsc{class}. This allows us to study the underlying physics. The response analysis carried out with it shows that $Ω_{Λ_3}$ is almost entirely absorbed by a shift in $h$ over the well-measured acoustic multipoles. The part of the signal that cannot be absorbed resides at low $\ell$, where cosmic variance dominates. Meanwhile, the $Ω_{Λ_2}$ signature is nearly degenerate with the overall amplitude of the spectrum. The effects of both parameters on the CMB power spectrum are small, since they represent higher-order thermal corrections to the cosmological constant. The resulting constraints are correspondingly weak: the induced shifts are largely absorbed by degeneracies with $h$ and with the overall normalization of the spectrum. This is the reason why neither parameter is detected.

</details>


### [155] [Constraints on Nonlinearity of the Mass-Concentration Relation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05662)
*Nobuhiro Okabe*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种贝叶斯方法，用于约束星系团质量-浓度（M-C）关系，综合考虑了内禀弥散、弱引力透镜校准非线性、测量误差协方差及母体质量分布，并在多个X射线选星系团样本上检验了多种M-C模型，结果倾向于具有正曲率的单调递减模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有M-C关系约束方法未充分整合观测与理论模型中的关键系统效应（如WL校准非线性、内禀弥散、误差协方差和质量函数），且不同理论/经验模型间缺乏统一贝叶斯比较框架。

Method: 构建贝叶斯统计框架，纳入浓度内禀弥散、非线性WL校准关系、WL测量误差协方差及父质量分布；采用哈密顿蒙特卡洛（HMC）高效采样；在eRASS1、XXL、LoCuSS和MCXC共213个X射线星系团上，按子样本分别处理其WL校准与质量分布；对比单幂律、双幂律及四种基于数值模拟的理论M-C模型。

Result: 所有模型推断出的M-C关系均与最新数值模拟结果一致；模型间无>4σ显著差异；但数据倾向支持具有正曲率（d²ln c/d(ln M)² > 0）的单调递减模型，该模型在2σ水平优于单幂律和负曲率单调模型，在0.5–1σ水平优于三种双幂律模型。

Conclusion: 当前观测数据（z<0.9，质量范围未达高红移/高质端上翘区）主要通过M-C关系曲率区分模型；正曲率单调递减模型最符合数据，暗示浓度随质量增加而缓慢趋平，为未来高精度巡天提供关键模型选择依据。

Abstract: We develop a Bayesian generalization of the previous method to constrain a wide variety of mass-concentration (M-C) relations motivated by both theoretical and observational studies. The framework incorporates the intrinsic scatter in concentration, nonlinear weak-lensing (WL) calibration relations, the WL measurement-error covariance, and the underlying parent mass distribution. We employ Hamiltonian Monte Carlo for computational efficiency. Our sample consists of 213 X-ray-selected clusters with WL measurements for the eRASS1, XXL, LoCuSS, and MCXC catalogs at 0<z< 0.9. For each subsample, we account for its specific WL calibration relation and underlying population distribution. We consider a conventional single power-law model, an empirical double power-law model, and four theoretical models calibrated against numerical simulations. The four theoretical M-C models comprise two in which concentration decreases with mass, distinguished by the sign of d^2ln c/d(ln M)^2, and two in which the concentration decreases with increasing mass before turning upward at high masses. Because the mass and redshift ranges of our cluster sample do not extend into the upturn regime predicted by numerical simulations, we distinguish among the models primarily through the curvature of their M-C relations. Regardless of which model is adopted, the inferred M-C relation is in good agreement with the results of recent numerical simulations. We find no statistically significant differences among the models above the $4σ$ level. However, the result shows a tendency toward a gradual flattening of the mass dependence toward higher masses. Specifically, a monotonically decreasing model with positive curvature is preferred over the single power-law relation and a monotonically decreasing model with negative curvature at the $2σ$ level and over three double power-law models at the $0.5-1σ$ level.

</details>


### [156] [Testing $Λ$LTB as an alternative to dynamical dark energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05729)
*Yukang Zhang,Pengjie Zhang*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在弱违反宇宙学原理（CP）的宇宙中，宇宙常数Λ如何模拟动力学暗能量（DDE），并利用ΛLTB模型与多组观测数据（如DESI、Planck、SNe Ia及kSZ效应）进行检验，发现虽大部分CP破缺参数空间被排除，但仍允许存在约300 Mpc/h尺度、约10%物质密度超量的区域，构成DDE的一种可行替代理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期观测报告的动力学暗能量（DDE）证据可能源于对宇宙学原理（CP）的微弱违背，而非真实物理演化；本文旨在检验宇宙常数Λ在弱CP破坏背景（如ΛLTB模型）下是否足以拟合当前观测数据，从而挑战DDE解释的必要性。

Method: 采用ΛLTB（Lambda-Lemaître-Tolman-Bondi）模型表征弱违反CP的各向异性宇宙，结合DESI DR2 BAO、Planck CMB、四组Ia型超新星（UNION3等）以及kSZ效应数据进行贝叶斯模型比对与参数约束，并推导了弱CP破坏下的距离-红移关系和kSZ信号解析表达式。

Result: （1）仅用BAO+CMB+SNe数据时，ΛLTB与DDE模型无显著优劣区分；（2）加入kSZ效应后，大幅压缩CP破缺参数空间，但仍容许一个尺度~300 Mpc/h、密度超量~10%的局部过密区存在；（3）给出了适用于进一步检验的弱CP破坏下距离-红移与kSZ的解析公式。

Conclusion: 当前观测数据尚不能完全排除‘Λ+弱CP破坏’作为DDE观测信号的几何/结构起源；该替代方案仍具可行性，需更精确的kSZ与大尺度结构数据进一步甄别。

Abstract: A cosmological constant $Λ$ in a universe weakly violating the cosmological principle (CP) can mimic a dynamical dark energy (DDE) in a FRW universe, and therefore challenging the recently reported DDE evidences. We test this scenario in the form of $Λ$LTB cosmology, against two data combinations. (1) DESI DR2 BAO, Planck CMB, together with any of the four SNe Ia compilations (UNION3, Pantheon+, DES SN5YR, and DES Dovekie) show no preference over DDE or $Λ$LTB. (2) The inclusion of the kinematic Sunyaev Zel'dovich (kSZ) effect rules out a large portion of the CP violation parameter space allowed by (1). However, the existence of a $\sim 300$ Mpc$/h$ region with $\sim 10\%$ matter density excess is still allowed by the current data, and therefore remains a viable alternative to DDE. To facilitate further tests, we present analytical expressions of the distance-redshift relation and the kSZ effect in a universe with weak CP violation.

</details>


### [157] [Limited Impact of CMB B-mode Polarization on Constraints on Cosmic Topology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05960)
*Ryota Himeno,Atsushi J. Nishizawa,Kiyotomo Ichiki*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究CMB B模偏振是否能提升对立方三维环面（E1拓扑）宇宙结构的限制能力，结果表明即使在理想无噪声条件下，B模的加入仅带来微弱改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宇宙全局空间结构是宇宙学基本问题，标准模型通常假设平凡拓扑，但多重连通拓扑可能破坏统计各向同性并产生CMB球谐系数的非对角协方差；需评估B模偏振是否增强对E1拓扑的探测能力。

Method: 构建温度、E模和原初B模各向异性的全谐空间协方差矩阵（含不同(ℓ,m)模式间相关性），在无仪器噪声与引力透镜的理想CVL条件下，使用ΛCDM各向同性模拟样本计算高斯似然。

Result: B模偏振对区分E1拓扑与平凡拓扑仅有微弱提升；不同观测量的约束能力差异源于其转移函数响应与拓扑诱导的谐空间非对角相关性的共同作用。

Conclusion: 原初B模偏振在探测宇宙拓扑结构方面价值有限，温度和E模仍是主要探针；拓扑信号的提取更依赖于多观测量联合建模与协方差结构理解。

Abstract: The global spatial structure of the universe is a fundamental question in cosmology. Although the standard model usually assumes a trivial topology, a multiply connected universe can break statistical isotropy and generate off-diagonal covariance between the spherical-harmonic coefficients of cosmic microwave background fluctuations. In this work, we investigate whether CMB B-mode polarization improves constraints on the cubic three-torus, or $E_1$ topology. We construct the full harmonic-space covariance matrices of the temperature, E-mode, and primordial B-mode anisotropies, including correlations between different $(\ell,m)$ modes, and evaluate Gaussian likelihoods using random realizations generated from a statistically isotropic $Λ$CDM model with trivial topology. To isolate the intrinsic topological information carried by each CMB observable, we consider the ideal cosmic-variance-limited case, neglecting instrumental noise and gravitational lensing. We find that adding primordial B-mode polarization yields only a marginal improvement in the ability to distinguish the $E_1$ topology from the trivial topology, even under these ideal conditions. The different constraining powers of the temperature, E-mode, and B-mode anisotropies can be understood through the combined effects of their transfer-function responses and the topology-induced off-diagonal correlations in harmonic space. }

</details>


### [158] [JESSNet: Joint dEconvolution and Sparse Separation Network for HI 21 cm foreground cleaning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06199)
*Athanasia Gkogkou,Victor Bonjean,Jean-Luc Starck,Pauline Gorbatchev,Marta Spinelli,Panagiotis Tsakalides*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出JESSNet，一种针对单口径射电望远镜21 cm强度映射的、具备波束感知与掩模兼容能力的前景清除框架，通过多尺度角分解、可学习球面小波正则化及掩模约束重建，在模拟SKA-MID数据中显著提升了中性氢信号恢复精度，尤其在低至中等角尺度和不完整天区覆盖下表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 21 cm强度映射中前景污染严重，尤其受色散仪器波束和不完整天区覆盖影响，需更鲁棒的前景清除方法。

Method: 基于SDecGMCA，引入三方面改进：多尺度角分解（含尺度依赖混合矩阵与通道选择）、可学习球面小波（'learnlet'）稀疏正则化、适用于银河系掩模与巡天脚印的掩模约束重建。

Result: 在SKA-MID类模拟数据（900–1300 MHz，含色散MeerKAT式波束、前景、噪声）上，JESSNet准确恢复中性氢角/频功率谱，优于单尺度方法，降低波束诱导谱残留，并在合成巡天脚印下仍保持功率谱保真度。

Conclusion: JESSNet是一种有效、灵活且开源的前景清除框架，适用于真实观测与模拟数据，为未来21 cm宇宙学研究提供可靠工具。

Abstract: Foreground cleaning is a major challenge for H\,{\sc i} 21 cm intensity mapping, particularly in the presence of a chromatic instrumental beam that couples angular foreground structure into the frequency direction and under incomplete sky coverage. We introduce JESSNet, a beam-aware, mask-compatible foreground-cleaning framework for single-dish intensity mapping. Building on SDecGMCA, JESSNet introduces three main extensions: a multiscale angular decomposition with scale-dependent mixing matrices and channel selection, a learned spherical-wavelet ''learnlet'' sparse regularization operator, and a mask-constrained reconstruction applicable to Galactic masks and survey footprints. We test JESSNet on simulated SKA-MID-like observations over $900\text{-}1300\,{\rm MHz}$ ($z\simeq0.09\text{-}0.58$), including H\,{\sc i} emission, Galactic and extragalactic foregrounds, thermal noise, and an oscillating MeerKAT-inspired chromatic beam. The multiscale reconstruction accurately recovers the angular and frequency power spectra of the input H\,{\sc i} signal, improves the recovery at low and intermediate angular scales relative to a single-scale implementation, and reduces residual beam-induced spectral features. JESSNet also remains effective for a synthetic survey footprint, preserving the H\,{\sc i} power spectra under incomplete sky coverage. The code and analysis pipeline are publicly released to facilitate reproducibility and future applications to simulated and observational data.

</details>


### [159] [Gravitational-wave background from supermassive black holes without merger trees: Tidally regulated mergers and direct inference from the NANOGrav 15 yr data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06220)
*Shin'ichiro Ando*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需蒙特卡洛模拟的半解析框架，直接计算超大质量黑洞双星产生的纳赫兹引力波背景，并利用NANOGrav 15年数据约束黑洞质量标度，发现其比本地标定高约0.31 dex。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决传统基于蒙特卡洛合并树方法计算引力波背景效率低、依赖预计算模型的问题，并解释NANOGrav观测到的较强纳赫兹引力波信号与本地黑洞-星系关系之间的张力。

Method: 采用N体校准的扩展Press-Schechter吸积核进行直接积分；耦合潮汐质量损失与钱德拉塞卡动力摩擦；引入卫星质量比阈值（ξ≈0.04）判断是否停滞；结合恒星硬化转折与偏心率谱，对五参数后验分布直接采样拟合NANOGrav 15年Hellings-Downs相关自由谱。

Result: 数据偏好黑洞质量归一化比本地黑洞-核球质量关系高+0.31^{+0.20}_{-0.23} dex；38%被吸积卫星在z=0仍未并合；谱形兼容但不需偏心双星或致密恒星环境；15.8 nHz异常功率被归因为脉冲星噪声。

Conclusion: 该半解析框架高效、无分辨率限制，能直接与PTA数据对比；结果稳健支持纳赫兹引力波背景强于本地标定的结论，且对延迟模型、合并核、本地标定及其红移演化不敏感。

Abstract: We present a semi-analytic framework that computes the gravitational-wave background (GWB) from supermassive black hole (SMBH) binaries by direct integration of an $N$-body-recalibrated extended Press-Schechter accretion kernel, bypassing Monte Carlo merger trees entirely. The calculation is resolution-free and fast enough to be sampled directly against pulsar-timing data with no emulator or precomputed model bank. Between halo accretion and black-hole binary formation, we couple the satellite's simulation-calibrated tidal mass loss continuously to Chandrasekhar dynamical friction. Satellites below a sharp, nearly redshift-independent mass-ratio threshold, $ξ\simeq 0.04$, are stripped faster than they sink and stall: their drag collapses onto the stellar core, and 38% of accreted satellites are unmerged by $z=0$. Combining this population model with per-binary stellar-hardening turnovers and eccentric spectra, we sample the five-parameter posterior directly against the NANOGrav 15 yr Hellings-Downs-correlated free spectrum, excluding the $15.8$ nHz bin, whose excess power a subsequent chromatic-noise reanalysis attributes to pulsar noise. The data prefer a black-hole mass normalization $+0.31^{+0.20}_{-0.23}$ dex above the locally calibrated black-hole-bulge mass relation, consistent with earlier indications that the nanohertz background is loud relative to local SMBH calibrations and robust to the delay model, merger kernel, local mass calibration, and its redshift evolution. The spectrum is compatible with, but does not require, eccentric binaries or dense stellar environments.

</details>


### [160] [Optimizing Color Selection for Cluster-Member Identification with SuperBIT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06635)
*Leila Ohashi,Sayan Saha,Jacqueline E. McCleary*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文利用SuperBIT望远镜2023年4月科学飞行获取的多波段成像数据，通过光谱证认的星系团成员，评估u-b、b-g、u-g三种颜色在红序星系团成员识别中的性能，发现b-g色指数具有最小的颜色弥散和最高纯度，因而被推荐为SuperBIT数据中基于红序识别星系团成员的首选颜色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 星系团丰富度图是示踪星系团恒星质量分布的有用工具，但仅依靠多波段测光数据识别星系团成员具有挑战性。

Method: 利用SuperBIT望远镜2023年4月科学飞行获得的三波段（u, b, g）成像数据，结合光谱证认的星系团成员样本，评估(u-b)、(b-g)、(u-g)三种颜色在红序成员识别中的表现，指标包括光谱成员在颜色空间的1σ弥散及成员识别算法的纯度。

Result: (b-g)色指数在SuperBIT所覆盖的红移范围内，在所有星系团样本中均展现出最小的颜色弥散和最高的识别纯度。

Conclusion: (b-g)色指数被确定为SuperBIT多波段测光数据中基于红序识别星系团成员的最优选择，为后续星系团星系种群分析提供依据。

Abstract: Galaxy richness maps provide a useful tracer of the stellar mass distribution in galaxy clusters, but identifying cluster members from multiband photometric data can be challenging. In this work, we investigate multiband imaging data obtained with the Super-Pressure Balloon-borne Imaging Telescope (SuperBIT) during its science flight in April 2023. Using spectroscopically identified cluster members across the SuperBIT cluster sample, we benchmark the performance of the three colors available from the three SuperBIT bands: $(u-b)$, $(b-g)$, and $(u-g)$. We quantify the performance of each color using the $1\,σ$ scatter of the spectroscopically identified members in color and the purity of our cluster-member identification algorithm. We find that $(b-g)$ consistently exhibits the smallest color scatter and highest purity across the sample and over the redshift range probed by SuperBIT. We therefore identify $(b-g)$ as the preferred color for red-sequence-based cluster-member identification, providing a basis for future analyses of the cluster galaxy population using SuperBIT multiband photometry.

</details>


### [161] [AI-assisted super-resolution cosmological simulations V: Cosmology-aware super-resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06710)
*Xiaowen Zhang,Patrick Lachance,Rupert A. C. Croft,Tiziana Di Matteo,Francisco Villaescusa-Navarro*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种宇宙学感知的生成模型，用于提升宇宙学N体模拟在粒子相空间中的超分辨率（SR），该模型联合生成位移和速度，并以低分辨率场和五个ΛCDM参数为条件，通过GAN实现跨宇宙学的高精度重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为提升宇宙学N体模拟的分辨率，同时保持对不同宇宙学参数的泛化能力，以支持非线性结构研究和场级宇宙学推断。

Method: 构建了一个基于生成对抗网络（GAN）的宇宙学感知超分辨率模型，联合生成粒子相空间中的位移与速度；以演化后的低分辨率场及五个ΛCDM参数（Ω_m, Ω_b, h, n_s, σ_8）为条件；在Quijote拉丁超立方套件的100组匹配低-高分辨率对上训练单个模型（512³→1024³粒子，1 h⁻¹ Gpc盒子，z=0）。

Result: 在10个未参与训练的宇宙学上，模型准确再现了物质分布、聚类与晕丰度的宇宙学依赖性；功率谱误差平均最大绝对值为3.6%（0.1<k<3 h Mpc⁻¹）；FoF晕丰度与高分辨率结果偏差≤15%（质量<10¹⁴·⁸ h⁻¹M⊙）；卫星丰度与占据数存在质量相关系统误差，但可通过单个近标称宇宙学校准显著缓解；功率谱推断测试显示校准可减小所有宇宙学参数的偏移均值。

Conclusion: 该模型成功实现了跨宇宙学的超分辨率重建，提供了高质量的6维相空间实现，拓展了SR方法在非线性结构建模与场级宇宙学推断中的应用潜力。

Abstract: We present a cosmology-aware generative model for super-resolution (SR) of cosmological $N$-body simulations in particle phase space. The model jointly generates displacements and velocities, conditioning a generative adversarial network (GAN) based generator and discriminator on evolved low-resolution (LR) fields and five $Λ$CDM parameters $(Ω_m,Ω_b,h,n_s,σ_8)$. We train a single model on 100 matched low-high resolution pairs from the Quijote Latin-hypercube suite at $z = 0$, mapping $512^3$ to $1024^3$ particles in $1\,h^{-1}\,\mathrm{Gpc}$ boxes. On ten cosmologies excluded from SR training, the model reproduces the broad matter distribution and the cosmological variation of clustering and halo abundances. Over $0.1<k<3\,h\,\mathrm{Mpc}^{-1}$, the mean of the per-cosmology maximum absolute power-spectrum errors is $3.6\%$, while FoF halo abundances agree with HR to within $15\%$ in the analyzed mass bins below $10^{14.8}\,h^{-1}M_\odot$. Satellite abundances and occupations retain larger, mass-dependent discrepancies. Corrections measured at one near-fiducial cosmology reduce a shared component of these systematic errors when applied to the other nine test cosmologies. A power spectrum based inference test also shows that calibration reduces the mean shifts in all parameters. These results extend our SR model across cosmologies and provide 6D phase space realizations for subsequent studies of non-linear structure and field-level cosmological inference.

</details>


### [162] [Testing Refracted Gravity with the kinematics of stacked galaxy clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06841)
*Dario Primignani,Lorenzo Pizzuti,Antonaldo Diaferio,Valeria Broccolato*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文测试了折射引力（RG）模型，通过分析74个星系团的成员星系运动学，利用MG-MAMPOSSt代码重构引力势并联合拟合RG参数与重子物质分布参数，发现真空介电常数ε₀在0.04–0.37之间，结果对热气体建模和样本选择具有一定敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验折射引力（RG）这一可替代暗物质的修正引力模型在星系团尺度上的有效性，探究其参数约束对重子物理建模和动力学非平衡效应的敏感性。

Method: 基于CIRS样本中74个星系团的成员星系速度弥散数据，按速度弥散分为三组堆叠以减小系统误差；使用MG-MAMPOSSt代码在RG框架下重建引力势，并联合拟合RG核心参数（特别是真空介电常数ε₀）及气体、恒星质量剖面参数，采用物理驱动的宽先验。

Result: 得到ε₀估计值在0.04至0.37之间，不同热气体模型下结果一致且与先前星系团尺度研究相容；当选用动力学更宁静的子样本时，结果对气体参数化依赖减弱。

Conclusion: RG模型在星系团尺度上具有可观测约束，但其参数精度显著依赖于热气体建模和样本的动力学状态选择，凸显了重子物理在修正引力检验中的关键作用。

Abstract: We test Refracted Gravity (RG), a phenomenological modified gravity model that modifies the Poisson equation by introducing an effective gravitational permittivity $ε(ρ_{\rm bar})$ which depends on the baryonic matter density $ρ_{\rm bar}$ and mimics the behaviour of dark matter in cosmic structures. We perform a kinematical analysis of member galaxies for a sample of 74 galaxy clusters, compiled from the Cluster Infall Regions in the Sloan Digital Sky Survey (CIRS). We stack the clusters in three bins of increasing velocity dispersion to mitigate the effects of systematic errors induced by deviations from dynamical equilibrium and from the assumption of spherical symmetry. By means of the \texttt{MG-MAMPOSSt} code, we reconstruct the gravitational potential in RG and infer the model parameters jointly with the gas and stellar mass-profile parameters, adopting broad, physically informed priors on the baryonic components. The estimates of the main parameter in Refracted Gravity, the vacuum permittivity $ε_0$, range from $0.04$ to $0.37$, across the three main stacks, depending on the model adopted for the hot gas component. The constraints are mutually compatible within the quoted uncertainties and overlap with previous cluster-scale estimates. The agreement becomes less sensitive to gas parametrisation when selecting the less-disturbed clusters for building the stacks. These results characterize the sensitivity of cluster-scale RG constraints to baryonic modelling and sample selection

</details>
